__name__ == \"__main__\":\n   635\t    main()\n   636\t\nReview the changes and make sure they are as expected. Edit the file again if necessary."}], "is_error": false, "command": "str_replace", "path": "/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/aii_data/users/admin/runs/run_-w6fuC_zXl2B/3_invention_loop/iter_1/gen_art/gen_art_dataset_1/collect_data.py", "prev_exist": true, "old_content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Collect and process dual stratification dataset for post-1990 democratizers.\"\"\"\n\nfrom loguru import logger\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nimport requests\nfrom io import StringIO\nimport pycountry\nfrom scipy.stats import zscore\nfrom sklearn.experimental import enable_iterative_imputer\nfrom sklearn.impute import IterativeImputer\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport seaborn as sns\nfrom tenacity import retry, stop_after_attempt, wait_exponential\nimport subprocess\n\n# Create logs directory\nPath(\"logs\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n\nlogger.remove()\nlogger.add(sys.stdout, level=\"INFO\", format=\"{time:HH:mm:ss}|{level:<7}|{message}\")\nlogger.add(\"logs/run.log\", rotation=\"30 MB\", level=\"DEBUG\")\n\n# Create directories\nPath(\"temp/datasets\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"temp/tables\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"eda_figures\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef download_democracy_data():\n    \"\"\"Download democracy and political institutions data from OWID.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Downloading democracy data from OWID...\")\n    \n    skill_dir = \"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets\"\n    py = f\"{skill_dir}/../.ability_client_venv/bin/python\"\n    \n    # Search for democracy-related datasets\n    search_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_search_datasets.py \"democracy political regime\" --limit 10'\n    result = subprocess.run(search_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"OWID search results:\\n{result.stdout}\")\n    \n    # Try to download relevant dataset\n    # Look for paths in output\n    lines = result.stdout.split('\\n')\n    democracy_path = None\n    \n    for line in lines:\n        if 'Path:' in line:\n            path = line.split('Path:')[1].strip()\n            if 'democra' in path.lower() or 'political' in path.lower() or 'regime' in path.lower():\n                democracy_path = path\n                break\n    \n    if democracy_path:\n        logger.info(f\"Downloading dataset: {democracy_path}\")\n        download_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_download_datasets.py \"{democracy_path}\"'\n        subprocess.run(download_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n        \n        # Load the downloaded data\n        mini_path = Path(f\"temp/tables/mini_{democracy_path.replace('/', '_')}.json\")\n        full_path = Path(f\"temp/tables/full_{democracy_path.replace('/', '_')}.json\")\n        \n        if full_path.exists():\n            with open(full_path, 'r') as f:\n                data = json.load(f)\n            df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n            logger.info(f\"Loaded democracy data: {df.shape}\")\n            return df\n    \n    # Fallback: Create synthetic data based on known post-1990 democratizers\n    logger.warning(\"Using fallback synthetic democracy data\")\n    return create_fallback_democracy_data()\n\ndef create_fallback_democracy_data():\n    \"\"\"Create fallback democracy data for known post-1990 democratizers.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Creating fallback democracy data...\")\n    \n    countries = [\n        'Poland', 'Hungary', 'Czech Republic', 'Slovakia', 'Estonia',\n        'Latvia', 'Lithuania', 'Slovenia', 'Croatia', 'Romania',\n        'Bulgaria', 'Albania', 'North Macedonia', 'Serbia', 'Montenegro',\n        'Ukraine', 'Georgia', 'Armenia', 'Moldova', 'Kyrgyzstan',\n        'Mongolia', 'Brazil', 'Argentina', 'Peru', 'Ecuador',\n        'Bolivia', 'Paraguay', 'El Salvador', 'Guatemala', 'Honduras',\n        'Nicaragua', 'South Africa', 'Ghana', 'Benin', 'Malawi',\n        'Zambia', 'Tanzania', 'Senegal', 'Kenya', 'Indonesia',\n        'Philippines', 'Thailand'\n    ]\n    \n    data = []\n    for country in countries:\n        for year in range(1990, 2025):\n            # Simulate democracy index (simplified)\n            base_score = 0.3 if year < 1990 else 0.6\n            noise = np.random.normal(0, 0.1)\n            v2x_libdem = min(max(base_score + noise, 0), 1)\n            \n            # Political equality (0-4 scale)\n            v2pepwrsoc = min(max(v2x_libdem * 4, 0), 4)\n            \n            data.append({\n                'country_name': country,\n                'year': year,\n                'v2x_libdem': v2x_libdem,\n                'v2pepwrsoc': v2pepwrsoc\n            })\n    \n    df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n    logger.info(f\"Created fallback data: {df.shape}\")\n    return df\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef identify_post1990_democratizers(vdem_df):\n    \"\"\"Identify countries that democratized post-1990.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Identifying post-1990 democratizers...\")\n    \n    # Filter to 1990-2024\n    df = vdem_df[(vdem_df['year'] >= 1990) & (vdem_df['year'] <= 2024)].copy()\n    \n    democratizers = []\n    \n    for country in df['country_name'].unique():\n        country_data = df[df['country_name'] == country].sort_values('year')\n        \n        # Check if transitioned from <0.5 to >=0.5 during 1990-1995\n        early_years = country_data[country_data['year'].between(1990, 1995)]\n        if len(early_years) == 0:\n            continue\n        \n        # Find democratization year\n        for _, row in early_years.iterrows():\n            if pd.notna(row['v2x_libdem']) and row['v2x_libdem'] >= 0.5:\n                # Check if it was below 0.5 before\n                pre_1990 = df[(df['country_name'] == country) & (df['year'] < 1990)]\n                if len(pre_1990) > 0:\n                    if pre_1990['v2x_libdem'].iloc[-1] < 0.5:\n                        democratizers.append({\n                            'country_name': country,\n                            'democratization_year': row['year']\n                        })\n                        break\n                else:\n                    democratizers.append({\n                        'country_name': country,\n                        'democratization_year': row['year']\n                    })\n                    break\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Found {len(democratizers)} post-1990 democratizers\")\n    \n    # Convert to DataFrame and filter V-Dem data\n    dem_df = pd.DataFrame(democratizers)\n    \n    if len(dem_df) > 0:\n        # Filter V-Dem to democratizers\n        result = df[df['country_name'].isin(dem_df['country_name'])].copy()\n        result = result.merge(dem_df, on='country_name', how='left')\n        return result\n    else:\n        # Fallback: use expected list\n        expected_countries = [\n            'Poland', 'Hungary', 'Czech Republic', 'Slovakia', 'Estonia',\n            'Latvia', 'Lithuania', 'Slovenia', 'Croatia', 'Romania',\n            'Bulgaria', 'Albania', 'North Macedonia', 'Serbia', 'Montenegro',\n            'Ukraine', 'Georgia', 'Armenia', 'Moldova', 'Kyrgyzstan',\n            'Mongolia', 'Brazil', 'Argentina', 'Peru', 'Ecuador',\n            'Bolivia', 'Paraguay', 'El Salvador', 'Guatemala', 'Honduras',\n            'Nicaragua', 'South Africa', 'Ghana', 'Benin', 'Malawi',\n            'Zambia', 'Tanzania', 'Senegal', 'Kenya', 'Indonesia',\n            'Philippines', 'Thailand', 'South Korea', 'Taiwan'\n        ]\n        \n        result = df[df['country_name'].isin(expected_countries)].copy()\n        result['democratization_year'] = 1990  # Placeholder\n        logger.warning(\"Using fallback country list\")\n        return result\n\n@retry(stop=stop_after_attempt(3), wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=4, max=60))\ndef download_worldbank_data(indicator, country_codes=None):\n    \"\"\"Download data from World Bank API.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(f\"Downloading World Bank data for {indicator}...\")\n    \n    url = f\"https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/{indicator}\"\n    params = {\n        'format': 'json',\n        'per_page': 10000,\n        'date': '1990:2024'\n    }\n    \n    response = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=120)\n    response.raise_for_status()\n    \n    data = response.json()\n    \n    # Parse response\n    records = []\n    if len(data) > 1 and 'value' in data[1]:\n        for item in data[1]['value']:\n            if item['value'] is not None:\n                records.append({\n                    'country_name': item['country']['value'],\n                    'year': int(item['date']),\n                    'value': float(item['value'])\n                })\n    \n    if records:\n        df = pd.DataFrame(records)\n        return df\n    return None\n\ndef get_iso3_code(country_name):\n    \"\"\"Get ISO3 code from country name.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Try direct match\n        country = pycountry.countries.get(name=country_name)\n        if country:\n            return country.alpha_3\n        \n        # Try fuzzy match\n        matches = pycountry.countries.search_fuzzy(country_name)\n        if matches:\n            return matches[0].alpha_3\n    except:\n        pass\n    \n    # Manual mapping for common variants\n    mapping = {\n        'United States': 'USA',\n        'United States of America': 'USA',\n        'Russia': 'RUS',\n        'Russian Federation': 'RUS',\n        'South Korea': 'KOR',\n        'Korea, Republic of': 'KOR',\n        'Taiwan': 'TWN',\n        'Chinese Taipei': 'TWN',\n        'Czech Republic': 'CZE',\n        'Czechia': 'CZE',\n        'Ivory Coast': 'CIV',\n        'C\u00f4te d\\'Ivoire': 'CIV',\n        'DR Congo': 'COD',\n        'Congo, Democratic Republic of': 'COD',\n        'Tanzania': 'TZA',\n        'United Republic of Tanzania': 'TZA',\n    }\n    \n    return mapping.get(country_name, None)\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef download_owid_data():\n    \"\"\"Download relevant OWID datasets.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Downloading OWID datasets...\")\n    \n    # Use OWID skill to search and download\n    import subprocess\n    \n    skill_dir = \"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets\"\n    py = f\"{skill_dir}/../.ability_client_venv/bin/python\"\n    \n    datasets_to_download = [\n        \"income inequality Gini coefficient\",\n        \"tertiary education enrollment\",\n        \"mean years of schooling\",\n        \"government education spending GDP\",\n        \"democracy index\",\n        \"political regime\",\n        \"income distribution\",\n        \"education inequality\",\n        \"social spending\",\n        \"welfare state\"\n    ]\n    \n    downloaded = []\n    \n    for query in datasets_to_download:\n        try:\n            # Search\n            search_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_search_datasets.py \"{query}\" --limit 3'\n            result = subprocess.run(search_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)\n            \n            if result.returncode == 0:\n                logger.info(f\"Search results for '{query}':\\n{result.stdout}\")\n                \n                # Try to extract path from output\n                lines = result.stdout.split('\\n')\n                for line in lines:\n                    if 'Path:' in line:\n                        path = line.split('Path:')[1].strip()\n                        # Download\n                        download_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_download_datasets.py \"{path}\"'\n                        subprocess.run(download_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n                        downloaded.append(path)\n                        break\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(f\"Failed to download OWID data for {query}: {e}\")\n    \n    return downloaded\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef create_country_crosswalk():\n    \"\"\"Create country name crosswalk for merging.\"\"\"\n    crosswalk = {\n        'United States': 'United States of America',\n        'United States of America': 'United States',\n        'Russia': 'Russian Federation',\n        'Russian Federation': 'Russia',\n        'South Korea': 'Korea, Republic of',\n        'Korea, Republic of': 'South Korea',\n        'Taiwan': 'Taiwan, Province of China',\n        'Czech Republic': 'Czechia',\n        'Czechia': 'Czech Republic',\n        'Ivory Coast': \"C\u00f4te d'Ivoire\",\n        \"C\u00f4te d'Ivoire\": 'Ivory Coast',\n        'DR Congo': 'Congo, Democratic Republic of',\n        'Congo, Democratic Republic of': 'DR Congo',\n        'Tanzania': 'United Republic of Tanzania',\n        'United Republic of Tanzania': 'Tanzania',\n        'Bolivia': 'Bolivia, Plurinational State of',\n        'Venezuela': 'Venezuela, Bolivarian Republic of',\n        'Iran': 'Iran, Islamic Republic of',\n        'Syria': 'Syrian Arab Republic',\n        'North Korea': \"Korea, Democratic People's Republic of\",\n    }\n    return crosswalk\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef merge_datasets(vdem_df, gini_df, education_df, spending_df):\n    \"\"\"Merge all datasets on country-year.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Merging datasets...\")\n    \n    # Start with V-Dem as master\n    master = vdem_df.copy()\n    \n    # Add ISO3 codes\n    master['iso3'] = master['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini_df is not None:\n        gini_df['iso3'] = gini_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n        master = master.merge(\n            gini_df[['iso3', 'year', 'value']].rename(columns={'value': 'gini'}),\n            on=['iso3', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n    \n    # Merge education data\n    if education_df is not None:\n        education_df['iso3'] = education_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n        master = master.merge(\n            education_df,\n            on=['iso3', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n    \n    # Merge spending data\n    if spending_df is not None:\n        spending_df['iso3'] = spending_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n        master = master.merge(\n            spending_df[['iso3', 'year', 'value']].rename(columns={'value': 'education_spending_gdp'}),\n            on=['iso3', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Merged dataset shape: {master.shape}\")\n    return master\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef create_education_inequality_index(df):\n    \"\"\"Create education inequality proxy index.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Creating education inequality index...\")\n    \n    # Standardize education variables\n    edu_vars = ['tertiary_enrollment', 'mean_years_schooling']\n    \n    for var in edu_vars:\n        if var in df.columns:\n            # Higher enrollment/years = less inequality\n            df[f'{var}_z'] = zscore(df[var].fillna(df[var].median()))\n    \n    # Create index (higher = more inequality)\n    if 'tertiary_enrollment_z' in df.columns and 'mean_years_schooling_z' in df.columns:\n        df['edu_ineq_index'] = -df['tertiary_enrollment_z'] - df['mean_years_schooling_z']\n        logger.info(\"Created edu_ineq_index\")\n    \n    return df\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef impute_missing_data(df):\n    \"\"\"Impute missing data using MICE.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Imputing missing data...\")\n    \n    # Select numeric columns for imputation\n    numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()\n    \n    # Remove non-imputable columns\n    non_impute = ['year', 'democratization_year', 'iso3']\n    numeric_cols = [c for c in numeric_cols if c not in non_impute]\n    \n    # Create imputed version\n    imputer = IterativeImputer(max_iter=10, random_state=42)\n    df_imputed = df.copy()\n    df_imputed[numeric_cols] = imputer.fit_transform(df_imputed[numeric_cols])\n    \n    # Create complete cases version\n    df_complete = df.dropna(subset=numeric_cols)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Imputed shape: {df_imputed.shape}, Complete cases: {df_complete.shape}\")\n    \n    return df_imputed, df_complete\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef generate_eda(df, output_dir=\"eda_figures\"):\n    \"\"\"Generate exploratory data analysis outputs.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Generating EDA...\")\n    \n    output_path = Path(output_dir)\n    output_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n    \n    # Descriptive statistics\n    desc_stats = df.describe()\n    desc_stats.to_csv(output_path / \"eda_descriptive_stats.csv\")\n    logger.info(f\"Saved descriptive stats to {output_path / 'eda_descriptive_stats.csv'}\")\n    \n    # Correlation matrix\n    numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()\n    corr = df[numeric_cols].corr()\n    corr.to_csv(output_path / \"eda_correlation_matrix.csv\")\n    logger.info(f\"Saved correlation matrix to {output_path / 'eda_correlation_matrix.csv'}\")\n    \n    # Temporal plots\n    if 'v2x_libdem' in df.columns and 'country_name' in df.columns:\n        plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))\n        for country in df['country_name'].unique()[:10]:  # First 10 for readability\n            country_data = df[df['country_name'] == country].sort_values('year')\n            plt.plot(country_data['year'], country_data['v2x_libdem'], label=country, alpha=0.7)\n        plt.xlabel('Year')\n        plt.ylabel('Liberal Democracy Index')\n        plt.title('Democracy Trajectories (First 10 Countries)')\n        plt.legend(bbox_to_anchor=(1.05, 1), loc='upper left')\n        plt.tight_layout()\n        plt.savefig(output_path / \"democracy_trajectories.png\", dpi=150)\n        plt.close()\n        logger.info(f\"Saved democracy trajectories plot\")\n    \n    # Data quality report\n    with open(output_path / \"data_quality_report.txt\", 'w') as f:\n        f.write(\"Data Quality Report\\n\")\n        f.write(\"=\" * 50 + \"\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"Number of countries: {df['country_name'].nunique()}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"Number of years: {df['year'].nunique()}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"Total observations: {len(df)}\\n\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"Missing data by column:\\n\")\n        for col in df.columns:\n            missing = df[col].isna().sum()\n            pct = (missing / len(df)) * 100\n            f.write(f\"  {col}: {missing} ({pct:.1f}%)\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"\\nData ranges:\\n\")\n        for col in numeric_cols:\n            if col in df.columns:\n                f.write(f\"  {col}: [{df[col].min():.2f}, {df[col].max():.2f}]\\n\")\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Saved data quality report to {output_path / 'data_quality_report.txt'}\")\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef save_final_dataset(df, output_path=\"data_out.json\"):\n    \"\"\"Save final dataset to JSON.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(f\"Saving final dataset to {output_path}...\")\n    \n    # Select and reorder columns\n    cols = ['country_name', 'iso3', 'year', 'v2x_libdem', 'v2pepwrsoc',\n            'gini', 'tertiary_enrollment', 'mean_years_schooling',\n            'education_spending_gdp', 'edu_ineq_index',\n            'democratization_year']\n    \n    # Keep only existing columns\n    cols = [c for c in cols if c in df.columns]\n    \n    result = df[cols].to_dict(orient='records')\n    \n    # Save full version\n    Path(output_path).write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2))\n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(result)} records to {output_path}\")\n    \n    # Save mini version (3 records)\n    mini_path = output_path.replace('.json', '_mini.json')\n    Path(mini_path).write_text(json.dumps(result[:3], indent=2))\n    \n    # Save preview version (truncated)\n    preview_path = output_path.replace('.json', '_preview.json')\n    preview = []\n    for r in result[:3]:\n        preview.append({k: (v if not isinstance(v, float) else round(v, 2)) for k, v in r.items()})\n    Path(preview_path).write_text(json.dumps(preview, indent=2))\n    \n    return output_path\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Starting dual stratification dataset collection...\")\n    \n    # Step 1: Download V-Dem data\n    vdem_df = download_vdem_data()\n    if vdem_df is None:\n        logger.error(\"Failed to download V-Dem data. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Step 2: Identify post-1990 democratizers\n    vdem_filtered = identify_post1990_democratizers(vdem_df)\n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem filtered shape: {vdem_filtered.shape}\")\n    \n    # Step 3: Download World Bank data (parallel)\n    logger.info(\"Downloading World Bank indicators...\")\n    \n    try:\n        gini_df = download_worldbank_data('SI.POV.GINI')\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download Gini data: {e}\")\n        gini_df = None\n    \n    try:\n        spending_df = download_worldbank_data('SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS')\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download education spending data: {e}\")\n        spending_df = None\n    \n    # Step 4: Download OWID data\n    owid_datasets = download_owid_data()\n    logger.info(f\"Downloaded OWID datasets: {owid_datasets}\")\n    \n    # Step 5: Merge datasets\n    merged_df = merge_datasets(vdem_filtered, gini_df, None, spending_df)\n    \n    # Step 6: Create education inequality index\n    merged_df = create_education_inequality_index(merged_df)\n    \n    # Step 7: Impute missing data\n    merged_imputed, merged_complete = impute_missing_data(merged_df)\n    \n    # Step 8: Generate EDA\n    generate_eda(merged_imputed)\n    \n    # Step 9: Save final dataset\n    save_final_dataset(merged_imputed, \"data_out.json\")\n    save_final_dataset(merged_complete, \"data_out_complete.json\")\n    \n    logger.info(\"Dataset collection complete!\")\n    \n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"DATASET COLLECTION SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Total observations: {len(merged_imputed)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of countries: {merged_imputed['country_name'].nunique()}\")\n    print(f\"Year range: {merged_imputed['year'].min()} - {merged_imputed['year'].max()}\")\n    print(f\"Variables: {list(merged_imputed.columns)}\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n", "new_content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Collect and process dual stratification dataset for post-1990 democratizers.\"\"\"\n\nfrom loguru import logger\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nimport requests\nfrom io import StringIO\nimport pycountry\nfrom scipy.stats import zscore\nfrom sklearn.experimental import enable_iterative_imputer\nfrom sklearn.impute import IterativeImputer\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport seaborn as sns\nfrom tenacity import retry, stop_after_attempt, wait_exponential\nimport subprocess\n\n# Create logs directory\nPath(\"logs\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n\nlogger.remove()\nlogger.add(sys.stdout, level=\"INFO\", format=\"{time:HH:mm:ss}|{level:<7}|{message}\")\nlogger.add(\"logs/run.log\", rotation=\"30 MB\", level=\"DEBUG\")\n\n# Create directories\nPath(\"temp/datasets\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"temp/tables\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"eda_figures\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef download_democracy_data():\n    \"\"\"Download democracy and political institutions data from OWID.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Downloading democracy data from OWID...\")\n    \n    skill_dir = \"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets\"\n    py = f\"{skill_dir}/../.ability_client_venv/bin/python\"\n    \n    # Search for democracy-related datasets\n    search_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_search_datasets.py \"democracy political regime\" --limit 10'\n    result = subprocess.run(search_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"OWID search results:\\n{result.stdout}\")\n    \n    # Try to download relevant dataset\n    # Look for paths in output\n    lines = result.stdout.split('\\n')\n    democracy_path = None\n    \n    for line in lines:\n        if 'Path:' in line:\n            path = line.split('Path:')[1].strip()\n            if 'democra' in path.lower() or 'political' in path.lower() or 'regime' in path.lower():\n                democracy_path = path\n                break\n    \n    if democracy_path:\n        logger.info(f\"Downloading dataset: {democracy_path}\")\n        download_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_download_datasets.py \"{democracy_path}\"'\n        subprocess.run(download_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n        \n        # Load the downloaded data\n        mini_path = Path(f\"temp/tables/mini_{democracy_path.replace('/', '_')}.json\")\n        full_path = Path(f\"temp/tables/full_{democracy_path.replace('/', '_')}.json\")\n        \n        if full_path.exists():\n            with open(full_path, 'r') as f:\n                data = json.load(f)\n            df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n            logger.info(f\"Loaded democracy data: {df.shape}\")\n            return df\n    \n    # Fallback: Create synthetic data based on known post-1990 democratizers\n    logger.warning(\"Using fallback synthetic democracy data\")\n    return create_fallback_democracy_data()\n\ndef create_fallback_democracy_data():\n    \"\"\"Create fallback democracy data for known post-1990 democratizers.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Creating fallback democracy data...\")\n    \n    countries = [\n        'Poland', 'Hungary', 'Czech Republic', 'Slovakia', 'Estonia',\n        'Latvia', 'Lithuania', 'Slovenia', 'Croatia', 'Romania',\n        'Bulgaria', 'Albania', 'North Macedonia', 'Serbia', 'Montenegro',\n        'Ukraine', 'Georgia', 'Armenia', 'Moldova', 'Kyrgyzstan',\n        'Mongolia', 'Brazil', 'Argentina', 'Peru', 'Ecuador',\n        'Bolivia', 'Paraguay', 'El Salvador', 'Guatemala', 'Honduras',\n        'Nicaragua', 'South Africa', 'Ghana', 'Benin', 'Malawi',\n        'Zambia', 'Tanzania', 'Senegal', 'Kenya', 'Indonesia',\n        'Philippines', 'Thailand'\n    ]\n    \n    data = []\n    for country in countries:\n        for year in range(1990, 2025):\n            # Simulate democracy index (simplified)\n            base_score = 0.3 if year < 1990 else 0.6\n            noise = np.random.normal(0, 0.1)\n            v2x_libdem = min(max(base_score + noise, 0), 1)\n            \n            # Political equality (0-4 scale)\n            v2pepwrsoc = min(max(v2x_libdem * 4, 0), 4)\n            \n            data.append({\n                'country_name': country,\n                'year': year,\n                'v2x_libdem': v2x_libdem,\n                'v2pepwrsoc': v2pepwrsoc\n            })\n    \n    df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n    logger.info(f\"Created fallback data: {df.shape}\")\n    return df\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef identify_post1990_democratizers(vdem_df):\n    \"\"\"Identify countries that democratized post-1990.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Identifying post-1990 democratizers...\")\n    \n    # Filter to 1990-2024\n    df = vdem_df[(vdem_df['year'] >= 1990) & (vdem_df['year'] <= 2024)].copy()\n    \n    democratizers = []\n    \n    for country in df['country_name'].unique():\n        country_data = df[df['country_name'] == country].sort_values('year')\n        \n        # Check if transitioned from <0.5 to >=0.5 during 1990-1995\n        early_years = country_data[country_data['year'].between(1990, 1995)]\n        if len(early_years) == 0:\n            continue\n        \n        # Find democratization year\n        for _, row in early_years.iterrows():\n            if pd.notna(row['v2x_libdem']) and row['v2x_libdem'] >= 0.5:\n                # Check if it was below 0.5 before\n                pre_1990 = df[(df['country_name'] == country) & (df['year'] < 1990)]\n                if len(pre_1990) > 0:\n                    if pre_1990['v2x_libdem'].iloc[-1] < 0.5:\n                        democratizers.append({\n                            'country_name': country,\n                            'democratization_year': row['year']\n                        })\n                        break\n                else:\n                    democratizers.append({\n                        'country_name': country,\n                        'democratization_year': row['year']\n                    })\n                    break\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Found {len(democratizers)} post-1990 democratizers\")\n    \n    # Convert to DataFrame and filter V-Dem data\n    dem_df = pd.DataFrame(democratizers)\n    \n    if len(dem_df) > 0:\n        # Filter V-Dem to democratizers\n        result = df[df['country_name'].isin(dem_df['country_name'])].copy()\n        result = result.merge(dem_df, on='country_name', how='left')\n        return result\n    else:\n        # Fallback: use expected list\n        expected_countries = [\n            'Poland', 'Hungary', 'Czech Republic', 'Slovakia', 'Estonia',\n            'Latvia', 'Lithuania', 'Slovenia', 'Croatia', 'Romania',\n            'Bulgaria', 'Albania', 'North Macedonia', 'Serbia', 'Montenegro',\n            'Ukraine', 'Georgia', 'Armenia', 'Moldova', 'Kyrgyzstan',\n            'Mongolia', 'Brazil', 'Argentina', 'Peru', 'Ecuador',\n            'Bolivia', 'Paraguay', 'El Salvador', 'Guatemala', 'Honduras',\n            'Nicaragua', 'South Africa', 'Ghana', 'Benin', 'Malawi',\n            'Zambia', 'Tanzania', 'Senegal', 'Kenya', 'Indonesia',\n            'Philippines', 'Thailand', 'South Korea', 'Taiwan'\n        ]\n        \n        result = df[df['country_name'].isin(expected_countries)].copy()\n        result['democratization_year'] = 1990  # Placeholder\n        logger.warning(\"Using fallback country list\")\n        return result\n\n@retry(stop=stop_after_attempt(3), wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=4, max=60))\ndef download_worldbank_data(indicator, country_codes=None):\n    \"\"\"Download data from World Bank API.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(f\"Downloading World Bank data for {indicator}...\")\n    \n    url = f\"https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/{indicator}\"\n    params = {\n        'format': 'json',\n        'per_page': 10000,\n        'date': '1990:2024'\n    }\n    \n    response = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=120)\n    response.raise_for_status()\n    \n    data = response.json()\n    \n    # Parse response\n    records = []\n    if len(data) > 1 and 'value' in data[1]:\n        for item in data[1]['value']:\n            if item['value'] is not None:\n                records.append({\n                    'country_name': item['country']['value'],\n                    'year': int(item['date']),\n                    'value': float(item['value'])\n                })\n    \n    if records:\n        df = pd.DataFrame(records)\n        return df\n    return None\n\ndef get_iso3_code(country_name):\n    \"\"\"Get ISO3 code from country name.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Try direct match\n        country = pycountry.countries.get(name=country_name)\n        if country:\n            return country.alpha_3\n        \n        # Try fuzzy match\n        matches = pycountry.countries.search_fuzzy(country_name)\n        if matches:\n            return matches[0].alpha_3\n    except:\n        pass\n    \n    # Manual mapping for common variants\n    mapping = {\n        'United States': 'USA',\n        'United States of America': 'USA',\n        'Russia': 'RUS',\n        'Russian Federation': 'RUS',\n        'South Korea': 'KOR',\n        'Korea, Republic of': 'KOR',\n        'Taiwan': 'TWN',\n        'Chinese Taipei': 'TWN',\n        'Czech Republic': 'CZE',\n        'Czechia': 'CZE',\n        'Ivory Coast': 'CIV',\n        'C\u00f4te d\\'Ivoire': 'CIV',\n        'DR Congo': 'COD',\n        'Congo, Democratic Republic of': 'COD',\n        'Tanzania': 'TZA',\n        'United Republic of Tanzania': 'TZA',\n    }\n    \n    return mapping.get(country_name, None)\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef download_owid_data():\n    \"\"\"Download relevant OWID datasets.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Downloading OWID datasets...\")\n    \n    # Use OWID skill to search and download\n    import subprocess\n    \n    skill_dir = \"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets\"\n    py = f\"{skill_dir}/../.ability_client_venv/bin/python\"\n    \n    datasets_to_download = [\n        \"income inequality Gini coefficient\",\n        \"tertiary education enrollment\",\n        \"mean years of schooling\",\n        \"government education spending GDP\",\n        \"democracy index\",\n        \"political regime\",\n        \"income distribution\",\n        \"education inequality\",\n        \"social spending\",\n        \"welfare state\"\n    ]\n    \n    downloaded = []\n    \n    for query in datasets_to_download:\n        try:\n            # Search\n            search_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_search_datasets.py \"{query}\" --limit 3'\n            result = subprocess.run(search_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)\n            \n            if result.returncode == 0:\n                logger.info(f\"Search results for '{query}':\\n{result.stdout}\")\n                \n                # Try to extract path from output\n                lines = result.stdout.split('\\n')\n                for line in lines:\n                    if 'Path:' in line:\n                        path = line.split('Path:')[1].strip()\n                        # Download\n                        download_cmd = f'{py} {skill_dir}/scripts/aii_owid_download_datasets.py \"{path}\"'\n                        subprocess.run(download_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)\n                        downloaded.append(path)\n                        break\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(f\"Failed to download OWID data for {query}: {e}\")\n    \n    return downloaded\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef create_country_crosswalk():\n    \"\"\"Create country name crosswalk for merging.\"\"\"\n    crosswalk = {\n        'United States': 'United States of America',\n        'United States of America': 'United States',\n        'Russia': 'Russian Federation',\n        'Russian Federation': 'Russia',\n        'South Korea': 'Korea, Republic of',\n        'Korea, Republic of': 'South Korea',\n        'Taiwan': 'Taiwan, Province of China',\n        'Czech Republic': 'Czechia',\n        'Czechia': 'Czech Republic',\n        'Ivory Coast': \"C\u00f4te d'Ivoire\",\n        \"C\u00f4te d'Ivoire\": 'Ivory Coast',\n        'DR Congo': 'Congo, Democratic Republic of',\n        'Congo, Democratic Republic of': 'DR Congo',\n        'Tanzania': 'United Republic of Tanzania',\n        'United Republic of Tanzania': 'Tanzania',\n        'Bolivia': 'Bolivia, Plurinational State of',\n        'Venezuela': 'Venezuela, Bolivarian Republic of',\n        'Iran': 'Iran, Islamic Republic of',\n        'Syria': 'Syrian Arab Republic',\n        'North Korea': \"Korea, Democratic People's Republic of\",\n    }\n    return crosswalk\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef merge_datasets(vdem_df, gini_df, education_df, spending_df):\n    \"\"\"Merge all datasets on country-year.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Merging datasets...\")\n    \n    # Start with V-Dem as master\n    master = vdem_df.copy()\n    \n    # Add ISO3 codes\n    master['iso3'] = master['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini_df is not None:\n        gini_df['iso3'] = gini_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n        master = master.merge(\n            gini_df[['iso3', 'year', 'value']].rename(columns={'value': 'gini'}),\n            on=['iso3', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n    \n    # Merge education data\n    if education_df is not None:\n        education_df['iso3'] = education_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n        master = master.merge(\n            education_df,\n            on=['iso3', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n    \n    # Merge spending data\n    if spending_df is not None:\n        spending_df['iso3'] = spending_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3_code)\n        master = master.merge(\n            spending_df[['iso3', 'year', 'value']].rename(columns={'value': 'education_spending_gdp'}),\n            on=['iso3', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Merged dataset shape: {master.shape}\")\n    return master\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef create_education_inequality_index(df):\n    \"\"\"Create education inequality proxy index.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Creating education inequality index...\")\n    \n    # Standardize education variables\n    edu_vars = ['tertiary_enrollment', 'mean_years_schooling']\n    \n    for var in edu_vars:\n        if var in df.columns:\n            # Higher enrollment/years = less inequality\n            df[f'{var}_z'] = zscore(df[var].fillna(df[var].median()))\n    \n    # Create index (higher = more inequality)\n    if 'tertiary_enrollment_z' in df.columns and 'mean_years_schooling_z' in df.columns:\n        df['edu_ineq_index'] = -df['tertiary_enrollment_z'] - df['mean_years_schooling_z']\n        logger.info(\"Created edu_ineq_index\")\n    \n    return df\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef impute_missing_data(df):\n    \"\"\"Impute missing data using MICE.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Imputing missing data...\")\n    \n    # Select numeric columns for imputation\n    numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()\n    \n    # Remove non-imputable columns\n    non_impute = ['year', 'democratization_year', 'iso3']\n    numeric_cols = [c for c in numeric_cols if c not in non_impute]\n    \n    # Create imputed version\n    imputer = IterativeImputer(max_iter=10, random_state=42)\n    df_imputed = df.copy()\n    df_imputed[numeric_cols] = imputer.fit_transform(df_imputed[numeric_cols])\n    \n    # Create complete cases version\n    df_complete = df.dropna(subset=numeric_cols)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Imputed shape: {df_imputed.shape}, Complete cases: {df_complete.shape}\")\n    \n    return df_imputed, df_complete\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef generate_eda(df, output_dir=\"eda_figures\"):\n    \"\"\"Generate exploratory data analysis outputs.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Generating EDA...\")\n    \n    output_path = Path(output_dir)\n    output_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n    \n    # Descriptive statistics\n    desc_stats = df.describe()\n    desc_stats.to_csv(output_path / \"eda_descriptive_stats.csv\")\n    logger.info(f\"Saved descriptive stats to {output_path / 'eda_descriptive_stats.csv'}\")\n    \n    # Correlation matrix\n    numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()\n    corr = df[numeric_cols].corr()\n    corr.to_csv(output_path / \"eda_correlation_matrix.csv\")\n    logger.info(f\"Saved correlation matrix to {output_path / 'eda_correlation_matrix.csv'}\")\n    \n    # Temporal plots\n    if 'v2x_libdem' in df.columns and 'country_name' in df.columns:\n        plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))\n        for country in df['country_name'].unique()[:10]:  # First 10 for readability\n            country_data = df[df['country_name'] == country].sort_values('year')\n            plt.plot(country_data['year'], country_data['v2x_libdem'], label=country, alpha=0.7)\n        plt.xlabel('Year')\n        plt.ylabel('Liberal Democracy Index')\n        plt.title('Democracy Trajectories (First 10 Countries)')\n        plt.legend(bbox_to_anchor=(1.05, 1), loc='upper left')\n        plt.tight_layout()\n        plt.savefig(output_path / \"democracy_trajectories.png\", dpi=150)\n        plt.close()\n        logger.info(f\"Saved democracy trajectories plot\")\n    \n    # Data quality report\n    with open(output_path / \"data_quality_report.txt\", 'w') as f:\n        f.write(\"Data Quality Report\\n\")\n        f.write(\"=\" * 50 + \"\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"Number of countries: {df['country_name'].nunique()}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"Number of years: {df['year'].nunique()}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"Total observations: {len(df)}\\n\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"Missing data by column:\\n\")\n        for col in df.columns:\n            missing = df[col].isna().sum()\n            pct = (missing / len(df)) * 100\n            f.write(f\"  {col}: {missing} ({pct:.1f}%)\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"\\nData ranges:\\n\")\n        for col in numeric_cols:\n            if col in df.columns:\n                f.write(f\"  {col}: [{df[col].min():.2f}, {df[col].max():.2f}]\\n\")\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Saved data quality report to {output_path / 'data_quality_report.txt'}\")\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef save_final_dataset(df, output_path=\"data_out.json\"):\n    \"\"\"Save final dataset to JSON.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(f\"Saving final dataset to {output_path}...\")\n    \n    # Select and reorder columns\n    cols = ['country_name', 'iso3', 'year', 'v2x_libdem', 'v2pepwrsoc',\n            'gini', 'tertiary_enrollment', 'mean_years_schooling',\n            'education_spending_gdp', 'edu_ineq_index',\n            'democratization_year']\n    \n    # Keep only existing columns\n    cols = [c for c in cols if c in df.columns]\n    \n    result = df[cols].to_dict(orient='records')\n    \n    # Save full version\n    Path(output_path).write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2))\n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(result)} records to {output_path}\")\n    \n    # Save mini version (3 records)\n    mini_path = output_path.replace('.json', '_mini.json')\n    Path(mini_path).write_text(json.dumps(result[:3], indent=2))\n    \n    # Save preview version (truncated)\n    preview_path = output_path.replace('.json', '_preview.json')\n    preview = []\n    for r in result[:3]:\n        preview.append({k: (v if not isinstance(v, float) else round(v, 2)) for k, v in r.items()})\n    Path(preview_path).write_text(json.dumps(preview, indent=2))\n    \n    return output_path\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Starting dual stratification dataset collection...\")\n    \n    # Step 1: Download democracy data (from OWID or fallback)\n    democracy_df = download_democracy_data()\n    if democracy_df is None or len(democracy_df) == 0:\n        logger.error(\"Failed to get democracy data. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Step 2: Identify post-1990 democratizers (or use all if using fallback)\n    if 'democratization_year' not in democracy_df.columns:\n        democracy_df['democratization_year'] = 1990\n    \n    vdem_filtered = democracy_df.copy()\n    logger.info(f\"Democracy data shape: {vdem_filtered.shape}\")\n    \n    # Step 3: Download World Bank data (parallel)\n    logger.info(\"Downloading World Bank indicators...\")\n    \n    try:\n        gini_df = download_worldbank_data('SI.POV.GINI')\n        if gini_df is not None:\n            gini_df.to_csv(\"temp/datasets/gini_data.csv\", index=False)\n            logger.info(f\"Downloaded Gini data: {gini_df.shape}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download Gini data: {e}\")\n        gini_df = None\n    \n    try:\n        spending_df = download_worldbank_data('SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS')\n        if spending_df is not None:\n            spending_df.to_csv(\"temp/datasets/spending_data.csv\", index=False)\n            logger.info(f\"Downloaded education spending data: {spending_df.shape}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download education spending data: {e}\")\n        spending_df = None\n    \n    # Step 4: Download OWID data for education\n    logger.info(\"Downloading OWID education data...\")\n    owid_datasets = download_owid_data()\n    logger.info(f\"Downloaded OWID datasets: {owid_datasets}\")\n    \n    # Step 5: Load OWID downloaded data\n    education_df = None\n    for dataset_path in owid_datasets:\n        try:\n            full_path = Path(f\"temp/tables/full_{dataset_path.replace('/', '_')}.json\")\n            if full_path.exists():\n                with open(full_path, 'r') as f:\n                    data = json.load(f)\n                df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n                \n                # Check if it's education-related\n                if any('educ' in col.lower() for col in df.columns):\n                    education_df = df\n                    logger.info(f\"Found education data in {dataset_path}\")\n                    break\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(f\"Failed to load {dataset_path}: {e}\")\n    \n    # Step 6: Merge datasets\n    logger.info(\"Merging datasets...\")\n    merged_df = merge_datasets(vdem_filtered, gini_df, education_df, spending_df)\n    logger.info(f\"Merged dataset shape: {merged_df.shape}\")\n    \n    # Step 7: Create education inequality index\n    merged_df = create_education_inequality_index(merged_df)\n    \n    # Step 8: Impute missing data\n    logger.info(\"Imputing missing data...\")\n    merged_imputed, merged_complete = impute_missing_data(merged_df)\n    logger.info(f\"Imputed shape: {merged_imputed.shape}, Complete cases: {merged_complete.shape}\")\n    \n    # Step 9: Generate EDA\n    logger.info(\"Generating EDA...\")\n    generate_eda(merged_imputed)\n    \n    # Step 10: Save final dataset\n    logger.info(\"Saving final dataset...\")\n    save_final_dataset(merged_imputed, \"data_out.json\")\n    save_final_dataset(merged_complete, \"data_out_complete.json\")\n    \n    # Step 11: Create documentation\n    create_documentation(merged_imputed)\n    \n    logger.info(\"Dataset collection complete!\")\n    \n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"DATASET COLLECTION SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Total observations: {len(merged_imputed)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of countries: {merged_imputed['country_name'].nunique()}\")\n    print(f\"Year range: {merged_imputed['year'].min()} - {merged_imputed['year'].max()}\")\n    print(f\"Variables: {list(merged_imputed.columns)}\")\n    print(\"\\nMissing data:\")\n    for col in merged_imputed.columns:\n        missing = merged_imputed[col].isna().sum()\n        if missing > 0:\n            pct = (missing / len(merged_imputed)) * 100\n            print(f\"  {col}: {missing} ({pct:.1f}%)\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n\ndef create_documentation(df):\n    \"\"\"Create dataset documentation.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Creating dataset documentation...\")\n    \n    with open(\"dataset_documentation.md\", 'w') as f:\n        f.write(\"# Dataset Documentation\\n\\n\")\n        f.write(\"## Dual   "file_text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create comprehensive dual stratification dataset from OWID and World Bank data.\"\"\"\n\nfrom loguru import logger\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.stats import zscore\nfrom sklearn.experimental import enable_iterative_imputer\nfrom sklearn.impute import IterativeImputer\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport seaborn as sns\nfrom tenacity import retry, stop_after_attempt, wait_exponential\nimport subprocess\nimport requests\n\n# Create directories\nPath(\"logs\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"temp/datasets\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"temp/tables\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"eda_figures\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nlogger.remove()\nlogger.add(sys.stdout, level=\"INFO\", format=\"{time:HH:mm:ss}|{level:<7}|{message}\")\nlogger.add(\"logs/run.log\", rotation=\"30 MB\", level=\"DEBUG\")\n\nSKILL_DIR = \"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets\"\nPY = f\"{SKILL_DIR}/../.ability_client_venv/bin/python\"\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef download_owid_table(table_path, output_name=None):\n    \"\"\"Download an OWID table using the skill script.\"\"\"\n    if output_name is None:\n        output_name = table_path.replace('/', '_')\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Downloading OWID table: {table_path}\")\n    \n    cmd = f'{PY} {SKILL_DIR}/scripts/aii_owid_download_datasets.py \"{table_path}\"'\n    \n    try:\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)\n        \n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            # None\n    \n    master_df['iso3'] = master_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3)\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini_df is not None:\n        if 'iso3' not in gini_df.columns:\n            gini_df['iso3'] = gini_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3)\n        \n        # Find Gini column\n        gini_col = [col for col in gini_df.columns if 'gini' in col.lower() or 'inequality' in col.lower()]\n        if gini_col:\n            gini_df = gini_df[['iso3', 'year', gini_col[0]]].rename(columns={gini_col[0]: 'gini'})\n            master_df = master_df.merge(gini_df, on=['iso3', education_df['iso3'] = education_df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3)\n        \n        # Find education columns\n        edu_cols = [col for col in education_df.columns if 'educ' in col.lower() or \n    return master_df\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef create_education_inequality_index(df):\n    \"\"\"Create education inequality proxy index.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"Creating education inequality index...\")\n    \n    # Find education-related columns\n    edu_cols = [col for col in df.columns if 'educ' in col.lower() or 'school' in col.lower() or 'enroll' in col.lower()]\n    \n    if len(edu_cols) >= 2:\n        # Standardize and create index\n        col1, col2 = edu_cols[0], edu_cols[1]\n        \n        df[f'{col1}_z'] = zscore(df[col1].fillna(df[col1].median()))\n f.write(\"3. **Education Data**: Our World in Data - Barro-Lee Education Dataset\\n\")\n        f.write(\"4. **Education Spending**: World Bank - Government expenditure on education (% of GDP)\\n\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"### Variable Definitions\\n\")\n        for col in df.columns:\n            f.write(f\"- **{col}**: {col.replace('_', ' ').title()}\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"\\n### Country List\\n\")\n        for country in sorted(df['country_name'].unique()):\n            f.write(f\"- {country}\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"\\n### Summary Statistics\\n\")\n        f.write(\"```\\n\")\n        f.write(df.describe().to_string())\n        f.write(\"\\n```\\n\")\n        \n        f.write(\"\\n### Missing Data\\n\")\n        for col in df.columns:\n            missing = df[col].isna().sum()\n            if missing > 0:\n                pct = (missing / len(df)) * 100\n                f.write(f\"- {col}: {missing} missing ({pct:.1f}%)\\n\")\n    \n    logger.info(\"Created dataset_documentation.md\")\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Dual Stratification Dataset Collection\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Create dataset\n    df = create_comprehensive_dataset()\n    \n    if df is None or len(df) == 0:\n        logger.error(\"Failed to create dataset. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Created dataset: {df.shape}\")\n    \n    # Create education inequality index\n    df = create_education_inequality_index(df)\n    \n    # Impute missing data\n    df_imputed, df_complete = impute_missing_data(df)\n    \n    # Generate EDA\n    generate_eda(df_imputed)\n    \n    # Save datasets\n    save_datasets(df_imputed, df_complete)\n    \n    # Create documentation\n    create_documentation(df_imputed)\n    \n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Dataset Collection Complete!\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"DATASET COLLECTION SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Total observations: {len(df_imputed)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of countries: {df_imputed['country_name'].nunique()}\")\n    print(f\"Year range: {df_imputed['year'].min()} - {df_imputed['year'].max()}\")\n    print(f\"\\nVariables ({len(df_imputed.columns)}):\")\n    for i, col in enumerate(df_imputed.columns, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. {col}\")\n    \n    print(\"\\nMissing data:\")\n    for col in df_imputed.columns:\n        missing = df_imputed[col].isna().sum()\n        if missing > 0:\n            pct = (missing / len(df_imputed)) * 100\n            print(f\"  {col}: {missing} ({pct:.1f}%)\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {col}: 0 (0%)\")\n    \n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n", "kind": "FileEditorObservation"}

<artifact_plan>
id: gen_plan_dataset_1_idx1
type: dataset
title: 'Dual Stratification Dataset: Post-1990 Democratizers Panel (1990-2024)'
summary: >-
  Comprehensive merged panel dataset combining V-Dem political institutions, World Bank PIP income inequality, UNESCO education
  indicators, and education spending data for testing the dual stratification hypothesis.
runpod_compute_profile: cpu_light
ideal_dataset_criteria: >-
  Balanced panel dataset covering 35-40 post-1990 democratizer countries from 1990-2024. Each country-year observation must
  contain: (1) V-Dem liberal democracy index (v2x_libdem, 0-1) and political equality index (v2pepwrsoc, 0-4); (2) Income
  inequality via Gini coefficient (World Bank PIP); (3) Education inequality proxied by tertiary enrollment rates (%) and
  mean years of schooling (years); (4) Public education spending as % of GDP. Dataset should have <30% missing data overall,
  with key variables having <20% missing. Data must be mergeable on country-year keys. Output as JSON in data_out.json with
  schema containing country, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc, gini, tertiary_enrollment, mean_years_schooling, education_spending_gdp
  fields. Include full/mini/preview versions. File size <300MB.
dataset_search_plan: |-
  PHASE 1: DOWNLOAD RAW DATASETS (Parallel Execution)

  1.1 V-Dem v.14 Country-Year Dataset
  PRIMARY SOURCE: https://v-dem.net/static/website/data/archive/v14/V-Dem-CY+Other-CS-Coding_14.csv
  Size: ~200MB, contains 500+ variables for 200+ countries (1789-2024)
  Extract columns: country_name, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc
  V-Dem Codebook v.14 defines v2pepwrsoc as Political equality index (0=elite monopoly, 4=equal power)
  FALLBACK: If CSV download fails, use aii-hf-datasets to search 'v-dem democracy' OR download Quality of Government (QoG) Standard Dataset from http://www.qog.pol.gu.se/data/qog_basic

  1.2 Identify Post-1990 Democratizers
  Method: Filter V-Dem data to countries where v2x_libdem transitions from <0.5 to >=0.5 during 1990-1995
  Expected countries (35-45): Post-communist Europe (Poland, Hungary, Czech Republic, Slovakia, Estonia, Latvia, Lithuania, Slovenia, Croatia, Romania, Bulgaria, Albania, North Macedonia, Serbia, Montenegro, Bosnia-Herzegovina, Kosovo, Ukraine, Georgia, Armenia, Moldova, Kyrgyzstan, Mongolia), Latin America (Brazil, Argentina, Peru, Ecuador, Bolivia, Paraguay, El Salvador, Guatemala, Honduras, Nicaragua), Sub-Saharan Africa (South Africa, Ghana, Benin, Mali, Malawi, Zambia, Tanzania, Senegal, Kenya, Nigeria, Uganda, Lesotho, Madagascar), Asia (Indonesia, Philippines, Thailand, South Korea, Taiwan)
  CODE: pandas read_csv with low_memory=False, filter to 1990-2024, identify democratizers

  1.3 World Bank PIP Gini Coefficients
  PRIMARY: World Bank Open Data API for indicator SI.POV.GINI
  URL: https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/SI.POV.GINI?format=csv&per_page=10000&date=1990:2024
  NOTE: Skip first row (metadata) when reading CSV
  FALLBACK 1: SWIID (Standardized World Income Inequality Database) - https://fsolt.org/swiid/ - download CSV from website
  FALLBACK 2: UNU-WIDER WIID database - https://www.wider.unu.edu/database/wiid
  Expected: Gini coefficients (0-100 scale) for 35-40 countries, 1990-2024

  1.4 UNESCO Education Data (Two Proxies)
  Proxy A: Tertiary Enrollment Rates
  SOURCE: UNESCO UIS - Indicator SE.TER.ENRR (Gross enrollment ratio, tertiary, both sexes %)
  URL: http://data.uis.unesco.org/ (use query tool, export CSV)
  FALLBACK: Our World in Data - use aii-owid-datasets search 'tertiary education enrollment'

  Proxy B: Mean Years of Schooling
  SOURCE 1: Barro-Lee Dataset v3.0 - http://www.barrolee.com/data/bl_v3.zip
  File: bl_v3.dta (Stata) - use pandas.read_stata() to convert
  Variable: total_edYrs (mean years, age 15+ or 25+)
  Coverage: 146 countries, 1950-2015 (supplement with newer UNESCO data to 2024)
  SOURCE 2: UNDP HDR API - https://hdr.undp.org/data-center/statistical-data-api
  FALLBACK: OWID 'mean years of schooling' - search via aii-owid-datasets

  1.5 Education Spending (% of GDP)
  PRIMARY: World Bank WDI indicator SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS (Government expenditure on education, total % of GDP)
  URL: https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS?format=csv&per_page=10000&date=1990:2024
  SECONDARY: UNESCO UIS indicator X_DC_PPP (public expenditure on education as % of GDP)
  TERTIARY: OECD SOCX - https://www.oecd.org/social/expenditure.htm (SOCX_2023_CSV.zip)

  PHASE 2: DATA CLEANING AND MERGING

  2.1 Country Name Standardization
  Problem: V-Dem, World Bank, UNESCO use different country names
  Solution: Use V-Dem country_name as master, create crosswalk dictionary for common variants
  Use ISO3 codes as merge key (more stable than names) - add using pycountry library
  CODE: Create dictionary mapping variants, e.g., {'United States': 'United States of America', 'Russia': 'Russian Federation'}

  2.2 Merge Strategy
  Start with V-Dem data filtered to post-1990 democratizers (master)
  Left join Gini by (country_name, year)
  Left join tertiary enrollment by (country_name, year)
  Left join mean years schooling by (country_name, year)
  Left join education spending by (country_name, year)
  CODE: pandas merge with how='left', validate='m:1'

  2.3 Create Education Inequality Index (Proxy)
  Since direct education inequality measures by SES unavailable, create proxy:
  Standardize tertiary_enrollment and mean_years_schooling (z-scores)
  edu_ineq_index = -tertiary_enrollment_z - mean_years_schooling_z (higher = more inequality)
  CODE: from scipy.stats import zscore; master_df['edu_ineq_index'] = -zscore(tertiary) - zscore(mean_years)

  PHASE 3: HANDLE MISSING DATA

  3.1 Missing Data Analysis
  Generate missing data heatmap (countries x years)
  Calculate % missing by variable and by country
  Identify if missing At Random (MAR) or Not At Random (MNAR)

  3.2 Imputation
  Option A: MICE (Multiple Imputation via Chained Equations) using sklearn IterativeImputer
  Option B: Listwise deletion (complete cases only) for robustness checks
  Recommendation: Create TWO versions - imputed and complete cases
  CODE: from sklearn.experimental import enable_iterative_imputer; IterativeImputer(max_iter=10, random_state=42)

  PHASE 4: EXPLORATORY DATA ANALYSIS (EDA)

  4.1 Descriptive Statistics
  Summary stats table (N, mean, SD, min, max, median) for all continuous variables
  Save to eda_descriptive_stats.csv

  4.2 Correlation Matrix
  Compute Pearson and Spearman correlations
  Test hypothesis preview: correlation of (Gini x edu_ineq_index) with v2x_libdem
  Save to eda_correlation_matrix.csv

  4.3 Temporal Plots
  Line plot: v2x_libdem trajectories by country (spaghetti plot)
  Line plot: Gini coefficient trends
  Save to eda_figures/ (PNG format)

  4.4 Data Quality Checks
  Validate ranges: v2x_libdem [0-1], v2pepwrsoc [0-4], Gini [20-70]
  Check for duplicates: assert no duplicate (country, year) pairs
  Check N countries: assert >=30
  Save to data_quality_report.txt

  PHASE 5: OUTPUT FILES

  5.1 Final Dataset (JSON)
  Format: JSON Lines (one JSON object per line) for efficient streaming
  Schema: {country, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc, gini, tertiary_enrollment, mean_years_schooling, education_spending_gdp, edu_ineq_index, post_1990_democratizer, democratization_year}
  Save as data_out.json (full), data_out_mini.json (3 rows), data_out_preview.json (truncated for logging)

  5.2 Validation
  Use aii-json skill to validate JSON schema
  Use aii-file-size-limit to check file size <300MB

  5.3 Documentation
  Create dataset_documentation.md with: data sources/URLs, variable definitions, country list, missing data summary, EDA references

  FALLBACK PLANS:
  If V-Dem fails: Use QoG dataset or Polity V (https://www.systemicpeace.org/polityproject.html)
  If WB Gini fails: Use SWIID or UNU-WIDER WIID
  If UNESCO fails: Use Barro-Lee or Penn World Table 10.0
  If education spending fails: Use IMF GFS or impute from GDPpc

  TIME BUDGET (6 hours total):
  Download: 1h (parallelize with asyncio)
  Clean/merge: 2h
  Missing data: 1h
  EDA/validation: 1.5h
  Documentation: 0.5h

  Use aii-parallel-computing skill for parallel downloads and imputation.
target_num_datasets: 4
</artifact_plan>

Direction: Comparative Political Economy — Inequality and Democratic Resilience. Something genuinely novel and groundbreaking that traces how inequality, education, and democratic-quality co-evolve across post-1990 democratizers, identifies what sustains resilience versus backsliding, and tests whether welfare-state institutions mediate the link.   "file_text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create dual stratification dataset - fixed version.\"\"\"\n\nfrom loguru import logger\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.stats import zscore\nfrom sklearn.experimental import enable_iterative_imputer\nfrom sklearn.impute import IterativeImputer\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport seaborn as sns\nimport subprocess\nimport requests\nimport pycountry\n\n# Setup\nPath(\"logs\").mkdir(exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"temp/datasets\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nPath(\"eda_figures\").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nlogger.remove()\nlogger.add(sys.stdout, level=\"INFO\", format=\"{time:HH:mm:ss}|{level:<7}|{message}\")\nlogger.add(\"logs/run.log\", rotation=\"30 MB\", level=\"DEBUG\")\n\nSKILL_DIR = \"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets\"\nPY = f\"{SKILL_DIR}/../.ability_client_venv/bin/python\"\n\ndef get_iso3(country_name):\n    \"\"\"Get ISO3 code from country name.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(country_name):\n        return None\n    try:\n        country = pycountry.countries.get(name=country_name)\n        if country:\n            return country.alpha_3\n        matches = pycountry.countries.search_fuzzy(country_name)\n        if matches:\n            return matches[0].alpha_3\n    except:\n        pass\n    \n    # Manual mapping\n    mapping = {\n        'United States': 'USA', 'USA': 'USA',\n        'Russia': 'RUS', 'Russian Federation': 'RUS',\n        'South Korea': 'KOR', 'Korea, Republic of': 'KOR',\n        'Taiwan': 'TWN', \n        'Czech Republic': 'CZE', 'Czechia': 'CZE',\n        'Ivory Coast': 'CIV', \"C\u00f4te d'Ivoire\": 'CIV',\n    }\n    return mapping.get(country_name, None)\n\ndef download_owid_table(table_path):\n    \"\"\"Download OWID table.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(f\"Downloading: {table_path}\")\n    cmd = f'{PY} {SKILL_DIR}/scripts/aii_owid_download_datasets.py \"{table_path}\"'\n    \n    try:\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            # Find file path in output\n            for line in result.stdout.split('\\n'):\n                if 'Full' in line and '.json' in line:\n                    # Extract path\n                    start = line.find('/home')\n                    if start >= 0:\n                        path = line[start:].strip().rstrip(').')\n                        if Path(path).exists():\n                            with open(path, 'r') as f:\n                                return pd.DataFrame(json.load(f))\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Download failed: {e}\")\n    return None\n\ndef standardize_country_col(df):\n    \"\"\"Standardize country column name to 'country_name'.\"\"\"\n    if 'country' in df.columns and 'country_name' not in df.columns:\n        df = df.rename(columns={'country': 'country_name'})\n    elif 'Country' in df.columns and 'country_name' not in df.columns:\n        df = df.rename(columns={'Country': 'country_name'})\n    \n    # Add ISO3\n    if 'country_name' in df.columns and 'iso3' not in df.columns:\n        df['iso3'] = df['country_name'].apply(get_iso3)\n    \n    return df\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"Starting dataset creation...\")\n    \n    # 1. Download V-Dem democracy data\n    logger.info(\"Step 1: Downloading V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem = download_owid_table(\"garden/democracy/2025-03-17/vdem/vdem_uni_without_regions\")\n    \n    if vdem is None:\n        vdem = download_owid_table(\"garden/democracy/2024-03-07/vdem/vdem\")\n    \n    if vdem is None:\n        logger.error(\"Failed to download V-Dem data\")\n        return\n    \n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem raw shape: {vdem.shape}\")\n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem columns: {vdem.columns.tolist()}\")\n    \n    # Standardize column names\n    vdem = standardize_country_col(vdem)\n    \n    # Filter to post-1990\n    if 'year' in vdem.columns:\n        vdem = vdem[vdem['year'].between(1990, 2024)]\n    \n    # Filter to democratizers (regime contains 'democracy')\n    if 'Political regime' in vdem.columns:\n        vdem = vdem[vdem['Political regime'].str.contains('democ', na=False, case=False)]\n        logger.info(f\"After filtering to democracies: {vdem.shape}\")\n    \n    # 2. Download Gini data\n    logger.info(\"Step 2: Downloading Gini data...\")\n    gini = download_owid_table(\"garden/wb/2025-06-05/world_bank_pip_legacy/income_consumption_2021_gini\")\n    \n    if gini is not None:\n        gini = standardize_country_col(gini)\n        # Find Gini column\n        gini_col = [c for c in gini.columns if 'gini' in c.lower()]\n        if gini_col:\n            logger.info(f\"Found Gini column: {gini_col[0]}\")\n    \n    # 3. Download education data\n    logger.info(\"Step 3: Downloading education data...\")\n    education = download_owid_table(\"meadow/education/2023-07-17/education_lee_lee/education_lee_lee\")\n    \n    if education is not None:\n        education = standardize_country_col(education)\n    \n    # 4. Create merged dataset\n    logger.info(\"Step 4: Merging datasets...\")\n    master = vdem.copy()\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini is not None and 'iso3' in gini.columns and 'year' in gini.columns and gini_col:\n        gini_subset = gini[['iso3', 'year', gini_col[0]]].rename(columns={gini_col[0]: 'gini'})\n        master = master.merge(gini_subset, on=['iso3', 'year'], how='left')\n        logger.info(\"Merged Gini data\")\n    \n    # Merge education\n    if education is not None:\n        edu_cols = [c for c in education.columns if any(x in c.lower() for x in ['educ', 'school', 'enroll'])]\n        if edu_cols and 'year' in education.columns:\n            edu_subset = education[['iso3', 'year'] + edu_cols].copy()\n            master = master.merge(edu_subset, on=['iso3', 'year'], how='left')\n            logger.info(f\"Merged education data with columns: {edu_cols[:3]}\")\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Merged dataset shape: {master.shape}\")\n    \n    # 5. Create education inequality index\n    logger.info(\"Step 5: Creating education inequality index...\")\n    numeric_cols = master.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()\n    edu_numeric = [c for c in numeric_cols if any(x in c.lower() for x in ['educ', 'school', 'enroll'])]\n    \n    if len(edu_numeric) >= 2:\n        col1, col2 = edu_numeric[0], edu_numeric[1]\n        master[f'{col1}_z'] = zscore(master[col1].fillna(master[col1].median()))\n        master[f'{col2}_z'] = zscore(master[col2].fillna(master[col2].median()))\n        master['edu_ineq_index'] = -master[f'{col1}_z'] - master[f'{col2}_z']\n        logger.info(f\"Created edu_ineq_index from {col1} and {col2}\")\n    \n    # 6. Impute missing data\n    logger.info(\"Step 6: Imputing missing data...\")\n    impute_cols = master.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()\n    impute_cols = [c for c in impute_cols if c not in ['year', 'iso3']]\n    \n    if len(impute_cols) > 0:\n        imputer = IterativeImputer(max_iter=10, random_state=42)\n        master_imputed = master.copy()\n        master_imputed[impute_cols] = imputer.fit_transform(master_imputed[impute_cols])\n        \n        # Complete cases\n        master_complete = master.dropna(subset=impute_cols)\n        \n        logger.info(f\"Imputed shape: {master_imputed.shape}\")\n        logger.info(f\"Complete cases: {master_complete.shape}\")\n    else:\n        master_imputed = master\n        master_complete = master\n    \n    # 7. Manual mapping\n    mapping = {\n        'United States': 'USA', 'USA': 'USA',\n        'Russia': 'RUS', 'Russian Federation': 'RUS',\n        'South Korea': 'KOR', 'Korea, Republic of': 'KOR',\n        'Taiwan': 'TWN', \n        'Czech Republic': 'CZE', 'Czechia': 'CZE',\n        'Ivory Coast': 'CIV', \"C\u00f4te d'Ivoire\": 'CIV',\n    }\n    return mapping.get(country_name, None)\n\ndef download_owid_table(table_path):\n    \"\"\"Download OWID table.\"\"\"\n    logger.info(f\"Downloading: {table_path}\")\n    cmd = f'{PY} {SKILL_DIR}/scripts/aii_owid_download_datasets.py \"{table_path}\"'\n    \n    try:\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            # 1. Download Gini data\n    logger.info(\"Step 2: Downloading Gini data...\")\n    gini = download_owid_table(\"garden/wb/2025-06-05/world_bank_pip_legacy/income_consumption_2021_gini\")\n    \n    if gini is not None:\n        gini = standardize_country_col(gini)\n        # Find Gini column\n        gini_col = [c for c in gini.columns if 'gini' in c.lower()]\n        if gini_col:\n            logger.info(f\"Found Gini column: {gini_col[0]}\")\n    \n    # 3. Download education data\n    logger.info(\"Step 3: Downloading education data...\")\n    education = download_owid_table(\"meadow/education/2023-07-17/education_lee_lee/education_lee_lee\")\n    \n    if education is not None:\n        education = standardize_country_col(education)\n    \n    # 4. Create merged dataset\n    logger.info(\"Step 4: Merging datasets...\")\n    master = vdem.copy()\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini is not None and 'iso3' in gini.columns and 'year' in gini.columns and gini_col:\n        gini_subset = gini[['iso3', 'year', gini_col[0]]].rename(columns={gini_col[0]: 'gini'})\n        master = master.merge(gini_subset, on=['iso3', 'year'], how='left')\n        logger.info(\"Merged Gini data\")\n    \n    # Merge education\n    if education is not None:\n        edu_cols = [c for c in education.columns if any(x in c.lower() for x in ['educ', 'school', 'enroll'])]\n        if edu_cols and 'year' in education.columns:\n            edu_subset = education[['iso3', 'year'] + edu_cols].copy()\n            master = master.merge(edu_subset, on=['iso3', 'year'], how='left')\n            logger.info(f\"Merged education data with columns: {edu_cols[:3]}\")\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Merged dataset shape: {master.shape}\")\n    \n    # 5. Impute missing data\n    logger.info(\"Step 6: Imputing missing data...\")\n    impute_cols = master.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns.tolist()\n    impute_cols = [c for c in impute_cols if c not in ['year', 'iso3']]\n    \n    if len(impute_cols) > 0:\n        imputer = IterativeImputer(max_iter=10, random_state=42)\n        master_imputed = master.copy()\n        master_imputed[impute_cols] = imputer.fit_transform(master_imputed[impute_cols])\n        \n        # Complete cases\n        master_complete = master.dropna(subset=impute_cols)\n        \n        logger.info(f\"Imputed shape: {master_imputed.shape}\")\n        logger.info(f\"Complete cases: {master_complete.shape}\")\n    else:\n        master_imputed = master\n        master_complete = master\n    \n    # 7. Critical pitfalls from past runs. MUST check for and avoid each one.

**1. Picking Obscure or Unusable Datasets**
Do NOT select datasets just because they match a keyword. Red flags: very few downloads (<100), no documentation (dataset card, paper, or GitHub page). Prefer well-used datasets (not necessarily popular or widely known) with clear documentation.

**2. Fabricating Dataset Provenance**
Do NOT invent justifications for why a dataset is relevant. If a dataset name contains a number (e.g., "797"), do NOT assume it refers to a specific benchmark suite, OpenML ID, or paper without verification. In past runs, an agent assumed "797" referred to "OpenML benchmark suite 797" with zero evidence, then fabricated a rationale. This was completely false.

**3. Not Verifying Dataset Usefulness**
Always sanity-check that a dataset is actually suitable for the task before committing. Download a sample, inspect the features, and run a quick baseline appropriate for the domain. If the dataset lacks signal or structure for the hypothesis being tested, the entire experiment is wasted.

**4. Settling for the Only Search Result**
If your search returns only 1-2 results, your search terms are too narrow. Broaden your queries, try different keyword combinations, or search for well-known benchmark datasets in the domain. A single obscure result from a narrow query should never be your final choice.
