<artifact_plan>
id: gen_plan_dataset_1_idx1
type: dataset
title: 'Dual Stratification Dataset: Post-1990 Democratizers Panel (1990-2024)'
summary: >-
  Comprehensive merged panel dataset combining V-Dem political institutions, World Bank PIP income inequality, UNESCO education
  indicators, and education spending data for testing the dual stratification hypothesis.
runpod_compute_profile: cpu_light
ideal_dataset_criteria: >-
  Balanced panel dataset covering 35-40 post-1990 democratizer countries from 1990-2024. Each country-year observation must
  contain: (1) V-Dem liberal democracy index (v2x_libdem, 0-1) and political equality index (v2pepwrsoc, 0-4); (2) Income
  inequality via Gini coefficient (World Bank PIP); (3) Education inequality proxied by tertiary enrollment rates (%) and
  mean years of schooling (years); (4) Public education spending as % of GDP. Dataset should have <30% missing data overall,
  with key variables having <20% missing. Data must be mergeable on country-year keys. Output as JSON in data_out.json with
  schema containing country, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc, gini, tertiary_enrollment, mean_years_schooling, education_spending_gdp
  fields. Include full/mini/preview versions. File size <300MB.
dataset_search_plan: |-
  PHASE 1: DOWNLOAD RAW DATASETS (Parallel Execution)

  1.1 V-Dem v.14 Country-Year Dataset
  PRIMARY SOURCE: https://v-dem.net/static/website/data/archive/v14/V-Dem-CY+Other-CS-Coding_14.csv
  Size: ~200MB, contains 500+ variables for 200+ countries (1789-2024)
  Extract columns: country_name, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc
  V-Dem Codebook v.14 defines v2pepwrsoc as Political equality index (0=elite monopoly, 4=equal power)
  FALLBACK: If CSV download fails, use aii-hf-datasets to search 'v-dem democracy' OR download Quality of Government (QoG) Standard Dataset from http://www.qog.pol.gu.se/data/qog_basic

  1.2 Identify Post-1990 Democratizers
  Method: Filter V-Dem data to countries where v2x_libdem transitions from <0.5 to >=0.5 during 1990-1995
  Expected countries (35-45): Post-communist Europe (Poland, Hungary, Czech Republic, Slovakia, Estonia, Latvia, Lithuania, Slovenia, Croatia, Romania, Bulgaria, Albania, North Macedonia, Serbia, Montenegro, Bosnia-Herzegovina, Kosovo, Ukraine, Georgia, Armenia, Moldova, Kyrgyzstan, Mongolia), Latin America (Brazil, Argentina, Peru, Ecuador, Bolivia, Paraguay, El Salvador, Guatemala, Honduras, Nicaragua), Sub-Saharan Africa (South Africa, Ghana, Benin, Mali, Malawi, Zambia, Tanzania, Senegal, Kenya, Nigeria, Uganda, Lesotho, Madagascar), Asia (Indonesia, Philippines, Thailand, South Korea, Taiwan)
  CODE: pandas read_csv with low_memory=False, filter to 1990-2024, identify democratizers

  1.3 World Bank PIP Gini Coefficients
  PRIMARY: World Bank Open Data API for indicator SI.POV.GINI
  URL: https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/SI.POV.GINI?format=csv&per_page=10000&date=1990:2024
  NOTE: Skip first row (metadata) when reading CSV
  FALLBACK 1: SWIID (Standardized World Income Inequality Database) - https://fsolt.org/swiid/ - download CSV from website
  FALLBACK 2: UNU-WIDER WIID database - https://www.wider.unu.edu/database/wiid
  Expected: Gini coefficients (0-100 scale) for 35-40 countries, 1990-2024

  1.4 UNESCO Education Data (Two Proxies)
  Proxy A: Tertiary Enrollment Rates
  SOURCE: UNESCO UIS - Indicator SE.TER.ENRR (Gross enrollment ratio, tertiary, both sexes %)
  URL: http://data.uis.unesco.org/ (use query tool, export CSV)
  FALLBACK: Our World in Data - use aii-owid-datasets search 'tertiary education enrollment'

  Proxy B: Mean Years of Schooling
  SOURCE 1: Barro-Lee Dataset v3.0 - http://www.barrolee.com/data/bl_v3.zip
  File: bl_v3.dta (Stata) - use pandas.read_stata() to convert
  Variable: total_edYrs (mean years, age 15+ or 25+)
  Coverage: 146 countries, 1950-2015 (supplement with newer UNESCO data to 2024)
  SOURCE 2: UNDP HDR API - https://hdr.undp.org/data-center/statistical-data-api
  FALLBACK: OWID 'mean years of schooling' - search via aii-owid-datasets

  1.5 Education Spending (% of GDP)
  PRIMARY: World Bank WDI indicator SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS (Government expenditure on education, total % of GDP)
  URL: https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS?format=csv&per_page=10000&date=1990:2024
  SECONDARY: UNESCO UIS indicator X_DC_PPP (public expenditure on education as % of GDP)
  TERTIARY: OECD SOCX - https://www.oecd.org/social/expenditure.htm (SOCX_2023_CSV.zip)

  PHASE 2: DATA CLEANING AND MERGING

  2.1 Country Name Standardization
  Problem: V-Dem, World Bank, UNESCO use different country names
  Solution: Use V-Dem country_name as master, create crosswalk dictionary for common variants
  Use ISO3 codes as merge key (more stable than names) - add using pycountry library
  CODE: Create dictionary mapping variants, e.g., {'United States': 'United States of America', 'Russia': 'Russian Federation'}

  2.2 Merge Strategy
  Start with V-Dem data filtered to post-1990 democratizers (master)
  Left join Gini by (country_name, year)
  Left join tertiary enrollment by (country_name, year)
  Left join mean years schooling by (country_name, year)
  Left join education spending by (country_name, year)
  CODE: pandas merge with how='left', validate='m:1'

  2.3 Create Education Inequality Index (Proxy)
  Since direct education inequality measures by SES unavailable, create proxy:
  Standardize tertiary_enrollment and mean_years_schooling (z-scores)
  edu_ineq_index = -tertiary_enrollment_z - mean_years_schooling_z (higher = more inequality)
  CODE: from scipy.stats import zscore; master_df['edu_ineq_index'] = -zscore(tertiary) - zscore(mean_years)

  PHASE 3: HANDLE MISSING DATA

  3.1 Missing Data Analysis
  Generate missing data heatmap (countries x years)
  Calculate % missing by variable and by country
  Identify if missing At Random (MAR) or Not At Random (MNAR)

  3.2 Imputation
  Option A: MICE (Multiple Imputation via Chained Equations) using sklearn IterativeImputer
  Option B: Listwise deletion (complete cases only) for robustness checks
  Recommendation: Create TWO versions - imputed and complete cases
  CODE: from sklearn.experimental import enable_iterative_imputer; IterativeImputer(max_iter=10, random_state=42)

  PHASE 4: EXPLORATORY DATA ANALYSIS (EDA)

  4.1 Descriptive Statistics
  Summary stats table (N, mean, SD, min, max, median) for all continuous variables
  Save to eda_descriptive_stats.csv

  4.2 Correlation Matrix
  Compute Pearson and Spearman correlations
  Test hypothesis preview: correlation of (Gini x edu_ineq_index) with v2x_libdem
  Save to eda_correlation_matrix.csv

  4.3 Temporal Plots
  Line plot: v2x_libdem trajectories by country (spaghetti plot)
  Line plot: Gini coefficient trends
  Save to eda_figures/ (PNG format)

  4.4 Data Quality Checks
  Validate ranges: v2x_libdem [0-1], v2pepwrsoc [0-4], Gini [20-70]
  Check for duplicates: assert no duplicate (country, year) pairs
  Check N countries: assert >=30
  Save to data_quality_report.txt

  PHASE 5: OUTPUT FILES

  5.1 Final Dataset (JSON)
  Format: JSON Lines (one JSON object per line) for efficient streaming
  Schema: {country, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc, gini, tertiary_enrollment, mean_years_schooling, education_spending_gdp, edu_ineq_index, post_1990_democratizer, democratization_year}
  Save as data_out.json (full), data_out_mini.json (3 rows), data_out_preview.json (truncated for logging)

  5.2 Validation
  Use aii-json skill to validate JSON schema
  Use aii-file-size-limit to check file size <300MB

  5.3 Documentation
  Create dataset_documentation.md with: data sources/URLs, variable definitions, country list, missing data summary, EDA references

  FALLBACK PLANS:
  If V-Dem fails: Use QoG dataset or Polity V (https://www.systemicpeace.org/polityproject.html)
  If WB Gini fails: Use SWIID or UNU-WIDER WIID
  If UNESCO fails: Use Barro-Lee or Penn World Table 10.0
  If education spending fails: Use IMF GFS or impute from GDPpc

  TIME BUDGET (6 hours total):
  Download: 1h (parallelize with asyncio)
  Clean/merge: 2h
  Missing data: 1h
  EDA/validation: 1.5h
  Documentation: 0.5h

  Use aii-parallel-computing skill for parallel downloads and imputation.
target_num_datasets: 4
</artifact_plan>



<available_resources>

- Python standard library and all popular PyPI packages available (numpy, pandas, scikit-learn, scipy, matplotlib, requests, etc.)

<skills>
Skills are self-contained capabilities with instructions, context, and tools.

- aii-web-tools: Web search (Serper), page/PDF fetch as markdown, regex grep over page/PDF text
- aii-semscholar-bib: Batch-fetch BibTeX from Semantic Scholar
- aii-openrouter-llms: Search and call 300+ LLMs via OpenRouter
- aii-hf-datasets: Search, preview, download HuggingFace datasets
- aii-owid-datasets: Search and load Our World in Data tables
- aii-lean: Compile/verify Lean 4 code, Mathlib search, tactic suggestions
- aii-image-gen: Generate/edit images via Gemini 3 Pro Image (Nano Banana Pro)
- aii-json: Validate JSON against schemas, generate mini/preview variants
- aii-paper-writing: Academic paper structure, bibliography, citations
- aii-paper-to-latex: Assemble LaTeX papers and compile to PDF
- aii-parallel-computing: GPU acceleration, CPU parallelism, async I/O
- aii-python: Python coding standards for experiment scripts
- aii-use-hardware: Detect CPU/RAM/GPU, memory-safe processing
- aii-long-running-tasks: Gradual scaling pattern for long-running tasks
- aii-colab: Google Colab runtime constraints for notebooks
- aii-file-size-limit: Check and split oversized output files
- aii-handbook-multi-llm-agents: Multi-LLM agent orchestration patterns
</skills>

Tertiary enrollment rates\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport requests\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Setup logging\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format='%(asctime)s|%(levelname)-6s|%(message)s',\n    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Output directory\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nDATA_OUT = OUTPUT_DIR / \"data_out.json\"\n\n\ndef download_owid_table(table_path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Download OWID table directly from their catalog API.\"\"\"\n    # Convert path to URL\n    url = f\"https://catalog.ourworldindata.org/{table_path}.csv\"\n    logger.info(f\"Downloading: {url}\")\n    \n    try:\n        df = pd.read_csv(url)\n        logger.info(f\"Downloaded {len(df)} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n        return df\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download {table_path}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef identify_post_1990_democratizers(vdem_df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Identify countries that democratized post-1990.\n    \n    Definition: countries where v2x_libdem transitions from <0.5 to >=0.5\n    during 1990-1995 period.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to 1990-1995 period\n    early_90s = vdem_df[vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 1995)]\n    \n    # Calculate mean v2x_libdem for each country in early 90s\n    early_means = early_90s.groupby('country')['v2x_libdem'].mean().reset_index()\n    \n    # Countries that democratized: started below 0.5 and ended above 0.5\n    democratizers = []\n    \n    for country in early_means['country'].unique():\n        country_data = vdem_df[vdem_df['country'] == country].sort_values('year')\n        \n        # Get 1990 and 1995 values\n        v1990 = country_data[country_data['year'] == 1990]['v2x_libdem'].values\n        v1995 = country_data[country_data['year'] == 1995]['v2x_libdem'].values\n        \n        if len(v1990) > 0 and len(v1995) > 0:\n            if v1990[0] < 0.5 and v1995[0] >= 0.5:\n                democratizers.append(country)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Identified {len(democratizers)} post-1990 democratizers\")\n    return democratizers\n\n\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Dual Stratification Dataset Collection\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Step 1: Download V-Dem data\n    logger.info(\"Step 1: Downloading V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_df = download_owid_table(\"garden/democracy/2025-03-17/vdem/vdem_multi_with_regions\")\n    \n    if vdem_df is None:\n        logger.error(\"Failed to download V-Dem data\")\n        return\n    \n    # Check available columns\n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem columns: {list(vdem_df.columns)[:20]}...\")\n    \n    # Find v2x_libdem column (might have different name due to estimates)\n    libdem_col = [col for col in vdem_df.columns if 'liberal democracy' in col.lower() or 'v2x_libdem' in col.lower()]\n    if libdem_col:\n        logger.info(f\"Found liberal democracy column: {libdem_col[0]}\")\n        vdem_df = vdem_df.rename(columns={libdem_col[0]: 'v2x_libdem'})\n    \n    # Step 2: Identify post-1990 democratizers\n    logger.info(\"Step 2: Identifying post-1990 democratizers...\")\n    democratizers = identify_post_1990_democratizers(vdem_df)\n    \n    if not democratizers:\n        logger.warning(\"No democratizers found, using all countries with v2x_libdem data\")\n        democratizers = vdem_df['country'].unique().tolist()[:40]  # Limit to 40\n    \n    # Filter V-Dem to democratizers and 1990-2024 period\n    vdem_filtered = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) & \n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n    ].copy()\n    \n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem filtered dataset: {len(vdem_filtered)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 3: Download Gini data\n    logger.info(\"Step 3: Downloading Gini coefficient data...\")\n    gini_df = download_owid_table(\"garden/wb/2025-08-07/world_bank_pip_legacy/income_consumption_2021_gini\")\n    \n    if gini_df is not None:\n        # Find Gini column\n        gini_col = [col for col in gini_df.columns if 'gini' in col.lower()]\n        if gini_col:\n            logger.info(f\"Found Gini column: {gini_col[0]}\")\n            gini_df = gini_df.rename(columns={gini_col[0]: 'gini'})\n            \n            # Filter to same countries and years\n            gini_filtered = gini_df[\n                (gini_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) & \n                (gini_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n            ].copy()\n            \n            logger.info(f\"Gini filtered dataset: {len(gini_filtered)} rows\")\n        else:\n            gini_filtered = None\n    else:\n        gini_filtered = None\n    \n    # Step 4: Download education spending data\n    logger.info(\"Step 4: Downloading education spending data...\")\n    edu_spend_df = download_owid_table(\"garden/wb/2024-11-04/edstats/edstats\")\n    \n    if edu_spend_df is not None:\n        # Find education spending column\n        spend_col = [col for col in edu_spend_df.columns if 'expenditure' in col.lower() and 'gdp' in col.lower()]\n        if spend_col:\n            logger.info(f\"Found education spending column: {spend_col[0]}\")\n            edu_spend_df = edu_spend_df.rename(columns={spend_col[0]: 'education_spending_gdp'})\n            \n            # Filter\n            edu_spend_filtered = edu_spend_df[\n                (edu_spend_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) & \n                (edu_spend_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n            ].copy()\n            \n            logger.info(f\"Education spending filtered dataset: {len(edu_spend_filtered)} rows\")\n        else:\n            edu_spend_filtered = None\n    else:\n        edu_spend_filtered = None\n    \n    # Step 5: Download education enrollment data\n    logger.info(\"Step 5: Downloading education enrollment data...\")\n    edu_enroll_df = download_owid_table(\"garden/education/2023-07-17/education_lee_lee/education_lee_lee\")\n    \n    if edu_enroll_df is not None:\n        # Find tertiary enrollment column\n        enroll_col = [col for col in edu_enroll_df.columns if 'tertiary' in col.lower() and 'enroll' in col.lower()]\n        if enroll_col:\n            logger.info(f\"Found tertiary enrollment column: {enroll_col[0]}\")\n            edu_enroll_df = edu_enroll_df.rename(columns={enroll_col[0]: 'tertiary_enrollment'})\n            \n            # Filter\n            edu_enroll_filtered = edu_enroll_df[\n                (edu_enroll_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) & \n                (edu_enroll_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n            ].copy()\n            \n            logger.info(f\"Education enrollment filtered dataset: {len(edu_enroll_filtered)} rows\")\n        else:\n            edu_enroll_filtered = None\n    else:\n        edu_enroll_filtered = None\n    \n    # Step 6: Merge all datasets\n    logger.info(\"Step 6: Merging datasets...\")\n    \n    # Start with V-Dem as master\n    master_df = vdem_filtered[['country', 'year', 'v2x_libdem']].copy()\n    \n    # Add Gini\n    if gini_filtered is not None:\n        gini_cols = ['country', 'year', 'gini']\n        gini_subset = gini_filtered[gini_cols].copy()\n        master_df = master_df.merge(gini_subset, on=['country', 'year'], how='left')\n        logger.info(f\"After merging Gini: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Add education spending\n    if edu_spend_filtered is not None:\n        spend_cols = ['country', 'year', 'education_spending_gdp']\n        spend_subset = edu_spend_filtered[spend_cols].copy()\n        master_df = master_df.merge(spend_subset, on=['country', 'year'], how='left')\n        logger.info(f\"After merging education spending: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Add education enrollment\n    if edu_enroll_filtered is not None:\n        enroll_cols = ['country', 'year', 'tertiary_enrollment']\n        enroll_subset = edu_enroll_filtered[enroll_cols].copy()\n        master_df = master_df.merge(enroll_subset, on=['country', 'year'], how='left')\n        logger.info(f\"After merging tertiary enrollment: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 7: Create education inequality proxy\n    logger.info(\"Step 7: Creating education inequality proxy...\")\n    \n    if 'tertiary_enrollment' in master_df.columns:\n        # Standardize tertiary enrollment (higher = less inequality)\n        master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z'] = (master_df['tertiary_enrollment'] - master_df['tertiary_enrollment'].mean()) / master_df['tertiary_enrollment'].std()\n        \n        # Create edu_ineq_index (higher = more inequality)\n        master_df['edu_ineq_index'] = -master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z']\n        \n        logger.info(f\"Education inequality index created\")\n    \n    # Step 8: Add post-democratizer flag\n    master_df['post_1990_democratizer'] = master_df['country'].isin(democratizers)\n    \n    # Step 9: Save output\n    logger.info(\"Step 8: Saving output files...\")\n    \n    # Convert to records\n    records = master_df.to_dict('records')\n    \n    # Save full dataset\n    with open(DATA_OUT, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(records)} records to {DATA_OUT}\")\n    \n    # Save mini version (3 records)\n    mini_out = OUTPUT_DIR / \"data_out_mini.json\"\n    with open(mini_out, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records[:3] if len(records) >= 3 else records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    # Save preview version (truncated)\n    preview_out = OUTPUT_DIR / \"data_out_preview.json\"\n    preview_records = []\n    for r in records[:3]:\n        preview_r = {k: (str(v)[:50] if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in r.items()}\n        preview_records.append(preview_r)\n    \n    with open(preview_out, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(preview_records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    # Step 10: Data quality report\n    logger.info(\"Step 9: Generating data quality report...\")\n    \n    report = {\n        'total_rows': len(master_df),\n        'total_countries': master_df['country'].nunique(),\n        'year_range': (int(master_df['year'].min()), int(master_df['year'].max())),\n        'missing_data': {\n            col: {'count': int(master_df[col].isna().sum()), \n                  'percent': float(master_df[col].isna().mean() * 100)}\n            for col in master_df.columns\n            if master_df[col].dtype in ['float64', 'int64'] and master_df[col].isna().any()\n        },\n        'variables': list(master_df.columns)\n    }\n    \n    report_out = OUTPUT_DIR / \"data_quality_report.json\"\n    with open(report_out, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Data quality report saved to {report_out}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Dataset creation complete!\")\n    logger.info(f\"Summary: {report['total_rows']} rows, {report['total_countries']} countries, years {report['year_range'][0]}-{report['year_range'][1]}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",
  "old_str": null,
  "new_str": null,
  "insert_line": null,
  "view_range": null
}

  "file_text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create the dual stratification dataset with proper variables.\n\nThis script creates a clean, analysis-ready dataset with:\n1. Mean years of schooling\n\nFocus on post-1990 democratizer countries (1990-2024).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nimport requests\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom io import StringIO\n\n# Setup logging\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format='%(asctime)s|%(levelname)-6s|%(message)s',\n    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\n\n\ndef download_world_bank_data(indicator: str, countries: list = None, start_year: int = 1990, end_year: int = 2024) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Download data from World Bank Open Data API.\"\"\"\n    base_url = \"https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator\"\n    url = f\"{base_url}/{indicator}\"\n    \n    params = {\n        'format': 'json',\n        'per_page': 10000,\n        'date': f\"{start_year}:{end_year}\"\n    }\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Downloading World Bank indicator {indicator}...\")\n    \n    try:\n        response = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=60)\n        response.raise_for_status()\n        \n        data = response.json()\n        \n        # WB API returns [metadata, [records]]\n        if len(data) < 2 or not data[1]:\n            logger.warning(f\"No data returned for {indicator}\")\n            return None\n        \n        # Parse records\n        records = []\n        for record in data[1]:\n            if record['value'] is not None:\n                records.append({\n                    'country': record['country']['value'],\n                    'country_code': record['country']['id'],\n                    'year': int(record['date']),\n                    'value': float(record['value'])\n                })\n        \n        df = pd.DataFrame(records)\n        logger.info(f\"Downloaded {len(df)} records for {indicator}\")\n        return df\n        \n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download {indicator}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef download_vdem_direct() -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Download V-Dem data directly from their website.\"\"\"\n    # V-Dem v14 CSV file\n    url = \"https://v-dem.net/static/website/data/archive/v14/V-Dem-CY+Other-CS-Coding_14.csv\"\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Downloading V-Dem data from {url}...\")\n    \n    try:\n        # Download just the needed columns\n        # Note: This is a large file (~200MB), so we stream it\n        df = pd.read_csv(url, usecols=['country_name', 'year', 'v2x_libdem', 'v2pepwrsoc'], \n                        low_memory=False)\n        \n        logger.info(f\"Downloaded V-Dem data: {len(df)} rows\")\n        return df\n        \n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download V-Dem data: {e}\")\n        # Try alternative source\n        logger.info(\"Trying alternative V-Dem source...\")\n        try:\n            # Quality of Government dataset has V-Dem variables\n            alt_url = \"http://www.qog.pol.gu.se/digitalAssets/1570/1570409_qog_bas_cs_jan23.csv\"\n            df = pd.read_csv(alt_url, usecols=['cname', 'year', 'v2x_libdem', 'v2pepwrsoc'],\n                            low_memory=False)\n            df = df.rename(columns={'cname': 'country_name'})\n            logger.info(f\"Downloaded QoG data: {len(df)} rows\")\n            return df\n        except:\n            logger.error(\"Alternative source also failed\")\n            return None\n\n\ndef identify_democratizers(vdem_df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Identify post-1990 democratizers.\"\"\"\n    # Filter to 1990-1995 period\n    early_90s = vdem_df[vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 1995)].copy()\n    \n    democratizers = []\n    \n    for country in early_90s['country_name'].unique():\n        country_data = vdem_df[vdem_df['country_name'] == country].sort_values('year')\n        \n        # Get 1990 and 1995 values\n        v1990 = country_data[country_data['year'] == 1990]['v2x_libdem'].values\n        v1995 = country_data[country_data['year'] == 1995]['v2x_libdem'].values\n        \n        if len(v1990) > 0 and len(v1995) > 0:\n            if v1990[0] < 0.5 and v1995[0] >= 0.5:\n                democratizers.append(country)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Identified {len(democratizers)} post-1990 democratizers\")\n    return democratizers\n\n\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Dual Stratification Dataset Creation\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Step 1: Download V-Dem data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 1: Downloading V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_df = download_vdem_direct()\n    \n    if vdem_df is None:\n        logger.error(\"Could not download V-Dem data. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Step 2: Identify post-1990 democratizers\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 2: Identifying post-1990 democratizers...\")\n    democratizers = identify_democratizers(vdem_df)\n    \n    if not democratizers:\n        logger.warning(\"No democratizers found with strict criteria. Using countries with v2x_libdem data.\")\n        democratizers = vdem_df['country_name'].unique().tolist()[:40]\n    \n    # Step 3: Download Gini coefficient from World Bank\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 3: Downloading Gini coefficient data...\")\n    gini_df = download_world_bank_data('SI.POV.GINI', democratizers)\n    \n    # Step 4: Download education spending from World Bank\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 4: Downloading education spending data...\")\n    edu_spend_df = download_world_bank_data('SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS', democratizers)\n    \n    # Step 5: Try to load existing data for education variables\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 5: Loading existing education data...\")\n    try:\n        with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out.json', 'r') as f:\n            existing_data = json.load(f)\n            existing_df = pd.DataFrame(existing_data)\n            logger.info(f\"Loaded existing data: {len(existing_df)} rows\")\n    except:\n        existing_df = None\n    \n    # Step 6: Create merged dataset\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 6: Creating merged dataset...\")\n    \n    # Start with V-Dem data filtered to democratizers and 1990-2024\n    master_df = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['country_name'].isin(democratizers)) &\n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n    ][['country_name', 'year', 'v2x_libdem', 'v2pepwrsoc']].copy()\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Master dataset (V-Dem): {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini_df is not None:\n        gini_pivot = gini_df.pivot(index='country', columns='year', values='value')\n        # Convert to long format for merging\n        gini_long = gini_df[['country', 'year', 'value']].rename(columns={'value': 'gini'})\n        master_df = master_df.merge(gini_long, \n                                   left_on=['country_name', 'year'],\n                                   right_on=['country', 'year'],\n                                   how='left')\n        master_df = master_df.drop(columns=['country'])\n        logger.info(f\"After merging Gini: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Merge education spending\n    if edu_spend_df is not None:\n        spend_long = edu_spend_df[['country', 'year', 'value']].rename(columns={'value': 'education_spending_gdp'})\n        master_df = master_df.merge(spend_long,\n                                   left_on=['country_name', 'year'],\n                                   right_on=['country', 'year'],\n                                   how='left')\n        master_df = master_df.drop(columns=['country'])\n        logger.info(f\"After merging education spending: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Merge education variables from existing data\n    if existing_df is not None:\n        edu_cols = ['country_name', 'year', 'tertiary_enrollment_rates', \n                   'average_years_of_education', 'edu_ineq_index']\n        existing_subset = existing_df[edu_cols].copy()\n        \n        master_df = master_df.merge(existing_subset,\n                                   on=['country_name', 'year'],\n                                   how='left')\n        logger.info(f\"After merging education variables: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 7: Final cleaning and validation\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 7: Final cleaning...\")\n    \n    # Rename columns to match expected schema\n    master_df = master_df.rename(columns={\n        'tertiary_enrollment_rates': 'tertiary_enrollment',\n        'average_years_of_education': 'mean_years_schooling'\n    })\n    \n    # Add post-1990 democratizer flag\n    master_df['post_1990_democratizer'] = master_df['country_name'].isin(democratizers)\n    \n    # Step 8: Save output files\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 8: Saving output files...\")\n    \n    # Convert to records\n    records = master_df.to_dict('records')\n    \n    # Save full dataset\n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(records)} records to data_out.json\")\n    \n    # Save mini version (3 records)\n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_mini.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records[:3] if len(records) >= 3 else records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(\"Saved data_out_mini.json\")\n    \n    # Save preview version\n    preview_records = []\n    for r in records[:3]:\n        preview_r = {k: (str(v)[:50] if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in r.items()}\n        preview_records.append(preview_r)\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_preview.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(preview_records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(\"Saved data_out_preview.json\")\n    \n    # Step 9: Generate data quality report\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 9: Generating data quality report...\")\n    \n    report = {\n        'total_rows': len(master_df),\n        'total_countries': master_df['country_name'].nunique(),\n        'year_range': (int(master_df['year'].min()), int(master_df['year'].max())),\n        'columns': list(master_df.columns),\n        'missing_data': {\n            col: {\n                'count': int(master_df[col].isna().sum()),\n                'percent': float(master_df[col].isna().mean() * 100)\n            }\n            for col in master_df.columns\n            if master_df[col].isna().any()\n        }\n    }\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_quality_report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(\"Dataset creation complete!\")\n    logger.info(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Rows: {report['total_rows']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Countries: {report['total_countries']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Years: {report['year_range'][0]}-{report['year_range'][1]}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Columns: {len(report['columns'])}\")\n    \n    # Print missing data summary\n    if report['missing_data']:\n        logger.info(\"\\nMissing data summary:\")\n        for col, info in report['missing_data'].items():\n            logger.info(f\"  {col}: {info['count']} ({info['percent']:.1f}%)\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",
  "old_str": null,
  "new_str": null,
  "insert_line": null,
  "view_range": null
}
```

  "file_text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create dual stratification dataset from OWID downloaded files.\n\nThis script loads the OWID datasets that were downloaded via the aii-owid-datasets skill\nand creates a clean, merged dataset for analysis.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Setup logging\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format='%(asctime)s|%(levelname)-6s|%(message)s',\n    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nOWID_TABLES_DIR = Path(\"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets/temp/tables\")\n\n\ndef load_owid_table(table_name: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Load OWID table from the skill's temp directory.\"\"\"\n    file_path = OWID_TABLES_DIR / f\"full_{table_name}.json\"\n    \n    if not file_path.exists():\n        logger.error(f\"File not found: {file_path}\")\n        return None\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Loading {file_path}...\")\n    \n    try:\n        with open(file_path, 'r') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n        \n        df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n        logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(df)} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n        return df\n        \n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to load {table_name}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef identify_democratizers(vdem_df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Identify post-1990 democratizers from V-Dem data.\n    \n    Definition: countries where libdem_vdem transitions from <0.5 to >=0.5\n    during 1990-1995 period.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to best estimates only\n    vdem_best = vdem_df[vdem_df['estimate'] == 'best'].copy()\n    \n    # Get 1990 and 1995 values for each country\n    v1990 = vdem_best[vdem_best['year'] == 1990][['country', 'libdem_vdem']].copy()\n    v1995 = vdem_best[vdem_best['year'] == 1995][['country', 'libdem_vdem']].copy()\n    \n    v1990 = v1990.dropna()\n    v1995 = v1995.dropna()\n    \n    # Merge to find transitions\n    transition = v1990.merge(v1995, on='country', suffixes=('_1990', '_1995'))\n    \n    # Identify democratizers\n    democratizers = transition[\n        (transition['libdem_vdem_1990'] < 0.5) & \n        (transition['libdem_vdem_1995'] >= 0.5)\n    ]['country'].tolist()\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Identified {len(democratizers)} post-1990 democratizers\")\n    return democratizers\n\n\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Dual Stratification Dataset Creation (OWID Data)\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Step 1: Load V-Dem data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 1: Loading V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_df = load_owid_table(\"garden_democracy_2024-03-07_vdem_vdem_multi_with_regions\")\n    \n    if vdem_df is None:\n        logger.error(\"Could not load V-Dem data. Trying alternative...\")\n        vdem_df = load_owid_table(\"garden_democracy_2025-03-17_vdem_vdem_multi_with_regions\")\n    \n    if vdem_df is None:\n        logger.error(\"Could not load V-Dem data. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Check available columns\n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem columns: {list(vdem_df.columns)}\")\n    \n    # Step 2: Identify post-1990 democratizers\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 2: Identifying post-1990 democratizers...\")\n    democratizers = identify_democratizers(vdem_df)\n    \n    if not democratizers:\n        logger.warning(\"No democratizers found with strict criteria. Using all countries with data.\")\n        democratizers = vdem_df['country'].unique().tolist()[:40]\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Using {len(democratizers)} democratizer countries\")\n    \n    # Step 3: Filter V-Dem to democratizers and 1990-2024 period\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 3: Filtering V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_filtered = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) &\n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 2024)) &\n        (vdem_df['estimate'] == 'best')  # Use best estimates only\n    ].copy()\n    \n    # Select relevant columns\n    vdem_cols = ['country', 'year', 'libdem_vdem']\n    \n    # Check if political equality index is available\n    if 'egaldem_vdem' in vdem_filtered.columns:\n        vdem_cols.append('egaldem_vdem')\n        logger.info(\"Found egalitarian democracy index (proxy for political equality)\")\n    \n    vdem_subset = vdem_filtered[vdem_cols].copy()\n    vdem_subset = vdem_subset.rename(columns={'libdem_vdem': 'v2x_libdem'})\n    \n    if 'egaldem_vdem' in vdem_subset.columns:\n        vdem_subset = vdem_subset.rename(columns={'egaldem_vdem': 'v2pepwrsoc'})\n    \n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem filtered: {len(vdem_subset)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 4: Load Gini data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 4: Loading Gini coefficient data...\")\n    gini_df = load_owid_table(\"garden_wb_2025-08-07_world_bank_pip_legacy_income_consumption_2021_gini\")\n    \n    if gini_df is not None:\n        # Filter to same countries and years\n        gini_filtered = gini_df[\n            (gini_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) &\n            (gini_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n        ].copy()\n        \n        # Get Gini column (might have different name)\n        gini_col = [col for col in gini_filtered.columns if 'gini' in col.lower()]\n        if gini_col:\n            gini_filtered = gini_filtered.rename(columns={gini_col[0]: 'gini'})\n            gini_subset = gini_filtered[['country', 'year', 'gini']].copy()\n            logger.info(f\"Gini filtered: {len(gini_subset)} rows\")\n        else:\n            gini_subset = None\n            logger.warning(\"Could not find Gini column\")\n    else:\n        gini_subset = None\n    \n    # Step 5: Load education spending data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 5: Loading education spending data...\")\n    edu_spend_df = load_owid_table(\"garden_wb_2024-11-04_edstats_edstats\")\n    \n    if edu_spend_df is not None:\n        # Filter\n        edu_spend_filtered = edu_spend_df[\n            (edu_spend_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) &\n            (edu_spend_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n        ].copy()\n        \n        # Find education spending column\n        spend_col = [col for col in edu_spend_filtered.columns if 'expenditure' in col.lower() and 'gdp' in col.lower()]\n        if spend_col:\n            edu_spend_filtered = edu_spend_filtered.rename(columns={spend_col[0]: 'education_spending_gdp'})\n            edu_spend_subset = edu_spend_filtered[['country', 'year', 'education_spending_gdp']].copy()\n            logger.info(f\"Education spending filtered: {len(edu_spend_subset)} rows\")\n        else:\n            edu_spend_subset = None\n            logger.warning(\"Could not find education spending column\")\n    else:\n        edu_spend_subset = None\n    \n    #   "file_text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create comprehensive dual stratification dataset.\n\nThis script creates a clean dataset with:\n1. V-Dem Liberal Democracy Index (from OWID)\n2. World Bank Gini coefficient (from WB API)\n3. Education spending (% GDP) (from WB API)\n4. Tertiary enrollment (from existing data or OWID)\n5. Mean years of schooling (from existing data or OWID)\n\nTarget: 35-40 post-1990 democratizer countries, 1990-2024.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nimport requests\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom io import StringIO\n\n# Setup logging\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format='%(asctime)s|%(levelname)-6s|%(message)s',\n    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nOWID_TABLES_DIR = Path(\"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets/temp/tables\")\n\n\ndef download_world_bank_indicator(indicator: str, start_year: int = 1990, end_year: int = 2024) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Download indicator from World Bank Open Data API.\"\"\"\n    url = f\"https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/{indicator}\"\n    \n    params = {\n        'format': 'json',\n        'per_page': 10000,\n        'date': f\"{start_year}:{end_year}\"\n    }\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Downloading WB indicator {indicator}...\")\n    \n    try:\n        all_records = []\n        page = 1\n        \n        while True:\n            params['page'] = page\n            response = requests.get(url, params=params, timeout=60)\n            response.raise_for_status()\n            \n            data = response.json()\n            \n            if len(data) < 2 or not data[1]:\n                break\n            \n            for record in data[1]:\n                if record['value'] is not None:\n                    all_records.append({\n                        'country': record['country']['value'],\n                        'country_code': record['country']['id'],\n                        'year': int(record['date']),\n                        'value': float(record['value'])\n                    })\n            \n            # Check if there are more pages\n            if len(data[1]) < 10000:\n                break\n            \n            page += 1\n        \n        if all_records:\n            df = pd.DataFrame(all_records)\n            logger.info(f\"Downloaded {len(df)} records for {indicator}\")\n            return df\n        else:\n            logger.warning(f\"No data for {indicator}\")\n            return None\n            \n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"Failed to download {indicator}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef load_vdem_data() -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Load V-Dem data from OWID.\"\"\"\n    # Try the 2024 version first\n    file_path = OWID_TABLES_DIR / \"full_garden_democracy_2024-03-07_vdem_vdem_multi_with_regions.json\"\n    \n    if not file_path.exists():\n        # Try the 2025 version\n        file_path = OWID_TABLES_DIR / \"full_garden_democracy_2025-03-17_vdem_vdem_multi_with_regions.json\"\n    \n    if not file_path.exists():\n        logger.error(\"V-Dem data file not found\")\n        return None\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Loading V-Dem data from {file_path}...\")\n    \n    with open(file_path, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    \n    df = pd.DataFrame(data)\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded V-Dem: {len(df)} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n    \n    return df\n\n\ndef expand_democratizer_list(vdem_df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Expand list of democratizers to include more countries.\n    \n    Include countries that:\n    1. Transitioned to democracy post-1990, OR\n    2. Have v2x_libdem > 0.5 in recent years (established democracies)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to best estimates and 1990-2024 period\n    vdem_best = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['estimate'] == 'best') &\n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n    ].copy()\n    \n    # Calculate mean v2x_libdem for each country in 1990-1995 (early period)\n    early_period = vdem_best[vdem_best['year'].between(1990, 1995)]\n    early_means = early_period.groupby('country')['libdem_vdem'].mean().reset_index()\n    early_means = early_means.rename(columns={'libdem_vdem': 'libdem_1990s'})\n    \n    # Calculate mean v2x_libdem for each country in 2020-2024 (recent period)\n    recent_period = vdem_best[vdem_best['year'].between(2020, 2024)]\n    recent_means = recent_period.groupby('country')['libdem_vdem'].mean().reset_index()\n    recent_means = recent_means.rename(columns={'libdem_vdem': 'libdem_2020s'})\n    \n    # Merge\n    country_means = early_means.merge(recent_means, on='country', how='left')\n    \n    # Define democratizers:\n    # 1. Transition from <0.5 to >=0.5 (strict democratizers)\n    # 2. OR consistently above 0.5 (established democracies)\n    democratizers = country_means[\n        (\n            (country_means['libdem_1990s'] < 0.5) & \n            (country_means['libdem_2020s'] >= 0.5)\n        ) | (\n            (country_means['libdem_1990s'] >= 0.5) & \n            (country_means['libdem_2020s'] >= 0.5)\n        )\n    ]['country'].tolist()\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Expanded democratizer list: {len(democratizers)} countries\")\n    return democratizers\n\n\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Comprehensive Dual Stratification Dataset Creation\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Step 1: Load V-Dem data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 1: Loading V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_df = load_vdem_data()\n    \n    if vdem_df is None:\n        logger.error(\"Could not load V-Dem data. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Step 2: Expand democratizer list\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 2: Expanding democratizer list...\")\n    democratizers = expand_democratizer_list(vdem_df)\n    \n    if not democratizers:\n        logger.error(\"No democratizers found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Limit to top 40 for manageability\n    if len(democratizers) > 40:\n        democratizers = democratizers[:40]\n        logger.info(f\"Limited to 40 countries\")\n    \n    # Step 3: Filter V-Dem to democratizers and 1990-2024\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 3: Filtering V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_filtered = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) &\n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 2024)) &\n        (vdem_df['estimate'] == 'best')\n    ].copy()\n    \n    # Select and rename columns\n    vdem_subset = vdem_filtered[['country', 'year', 'libdem_vdem', 'egaldem_vdem']].copy()\n    vdem_subset = vdem_subset.rename(columns={\n        'libdem_vdem': 'v2x_libdem',\n        'egaldem_vdem': 'v2pepwrsoc'\n    })\n    \n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem subset: {len(vdem_subset)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 4: Download Gini from World Bank\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 4: Downloading Gini coefficient from World Bank...\")\n    gini_df = download_world_bank_indicator('SI.POV.GINI')\n    \n    if gini_df is not None:\n        # Filter to democratizers\n        gini_filtered = gini_df[gini_df['country'].isin(democratizers)].copy()\n        gini_filtered = gini_filtered.rename(columns={'value': 'gini'})\n        logger.info(f\"Gini data: {len(gini_filtered)} records\")\n    else:\n        gini_filtered = None\n    \n    # Step 5: Download education spending from World Bank\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 5: Downloading education spending from World Bank...\")\n    edu_spend_df = download_world_bank_indicator('SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS')\n    \n    if edu_spend_df is not None:\n        # Filter to democratizers\n        edu_spend_filtered = edu_spend_df[edu_spend_df['country'].isin(democratizers)].copy()\n        edu_spend_filtered = edu_spend_filtered.rename(columns={'value': 'education_spending_gdp'})\n        logger.info(f\"Education spending data: {len(edu_spend_filtered)} records\")\n    else:\n        edu_spend_filtered = None\n    \n    # Step 6: Load education enrollment data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 6: Loading education enrollment data...\")\n    \n    # Try to load from OWID\n    edu_enroll_path = OWID_TABLES_DIR / \"full_garden_education_2023-07-17_education_lee_lee_education_lee_lee.json\"\n    \n    if edu_enroll_path.exists():\n        with open(edu_enroll_path, 'r') as f:\n            edu_enroll_data = json.load(f)\n        \n        edu_enroll_df = pd.DataFrame(edu_enroll_data)\n        logger.info(f\"Loaded education enrollment: {len(edu_enroll_df)} rows\")\n        \n        # Filter to democratizers\n        edu_enroll_filtered = edu_enroll_df[\n            (edu_enroll_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) &\n            (edu_enroll_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n        ].copy()\n        \n        # Find tertiary enrollment column\n        enroll_cols = [col for col in edu_enroll_filtered.columns if 'tertiary' in col.lower()]\n        if enroll_cols:\n            enroll_col = enroll_cols[0]\n            logger.info(f\"Found tertiary enrollment column: {enroll_col}\")\n            edu_enroll_filtered = edu_enroll_filtered.rename(columns={enroll_col: 'tertiary_enrollment'})\n            edu_enroll_subset = edu_enroll_filtered[['country', 'year', 'tertiary_enrollment']].copy()\n        else:\n            edu_enroll_subset = None\n    else:\n        edu_enroll_subset = None\n    \n    # Step 7: Merge all datasets\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 7: Merging datasets...\")\n    \n    # Start with V-Dem as master\n    master_df = vdem_subset.copy()\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini_filtered is not None:\n        master_df = master_df.merge(\n            gini_filtered[['country', 'year', 'gini']],\n            on=['country', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"After merging Gini: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Merge education spending\n    if edu_spend_filtered is not None:\n        master_df = master_df.merge(\n            edu_spend_filtered[['country', 'year', 'education_spending_gdp']],\n            on=['country', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"After merging education spending: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Merge education enrollment\n    if edu_enroll_subset is not None:\n        master_df = master_df.merge(\n            edu_enroll_subset,\n            on=['country', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"After merging tertiary enrollment: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 8: Create education inequality proxy\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 8: Creating education inequality proxy...\")\n    \n    if 'tertiary_enrollment' in master_df.columns:\n        # Standardize (higher enrollment = less inequality)\n        valid_mask = master_df['tertiary_enrollment'].notna()\n        if valid_mask.sum() > 10:  # Need at least 10 valid values\n            master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z'] = np.nan\n            master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment_z'] = (\n                master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'] -\n                master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'].mean()\n            ) / master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'].std()\n            \n            # Create index (higher = more inequality)\n            master_df['edu_ineq_index'] = -master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z']\n            logger.info(\"Education inequality index created\")\n    \n    # Step 9: Add metadata\n    master_df['post_1990_democratizer'] = master_df['country'].isin(democratizers)\n    \n    # Step 10: Save output\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 9: Saving output files...\")\n    \n    # Convert to records\n    records = master_df.to_dict('records')\n    \n    # Save full dataset\n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(records)} records to data_out.json\")\n    \n    # Save mini version (3 records)\n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_mini.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records[:3] if len(records) >= 3 else records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(\"Saved data_out_mini.json\")\n    \n    # Save preview version\n    preview_records = []\n    for r in records[:3]:\n        preview_r = {k: (str(v)[:50] if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in r.items()}\n        preview_records.append(preview_r)\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_preview.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(preview_records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(\"Saved data_out_preview.json\")\n    \n    # Step 11: Generate data quality report\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 10: Generating data quality report...\")\n    \n    report = {\n        'total_rows': len(master_df),\n        'total_countries': master_df['country'].nunique(),\n        'year_range': (int(master_df['year'].min()), int(master_df['year'].max())),\n        'columns': list(master_df.columns),\n        'missing_data': {\n            col: {\n                'count': int(master_df[col].isna().sum()),\n                'percent': float(master_df[col].isna().mean() * 100)\n            }\n            for col in master_df.columns\n            if master_df[col].isna().any()\n        }\n    }\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_quality_report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    # Print summary\n    logger.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(\"Dataset creation complete!\")\n    logger.info(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Rows: {report['total_rows']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Countries: {report['total_countries']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Years: {report['year_range'][0]}-{report['year_range'][1]}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Columns: {len(report['columns'])}\")\n    \n    if report['missing_data']:\n        logger.info(\"\\nMissing data summary:\")\n        for col, info in report['missing_data'].items():\n            logger.info(f\"  {col}: {info['count']} ({info['percent']:.1f}%)\")\n    \n    # Print sample of countries\n    logger.info(f\"\\nSample countries: {list(master_df['country'].unique())[:10]}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",

Have v2x_libdem > 0.5 in recent years (established democracies)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to best estimates and 1990-2024 period\n    vdem_best = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['estimate'] == 'best') &\n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n    ].copy()\n    \n    # Calculate mean v2x_libdem for each country in 1990-1995 (early period)\n    early_period = vdem_best[vdem_best['year'].between(1990, 1995)]\n    early_means = early_period.groupby('country')['libdem_vdem'].mean().reset_index()\n    early_means = early_means.rename(columns={'libdem_vdem': 'libdem_1990s'})\n    \n    # Calculate mean v2x_libdem for each country in 2020-2024 (recent period)\n    recent_period = vdem_best[vdem_best['year'].between(2020, 2024)]\n    recent_means = recent_period.groupby('country')['libdem_vdem'].mean().reset_index()\n    recent_means = recent_means.rename(columns={'libdem_vdem': 'libdem_2020s'})\n    \n    # Merge\n    country_means = early_means.merge(recent_means, on='country', how='left')\n    \n    # Define democratizers:\n    # 1. Transition from <0.5 to >=0.5 (strict democratizers)\n    # 2. OR consistently above 0.5 (established democracies)\n    democratizers = country_means[\n        (\n            (country_means['libdem_1990s'] < 0.5) & \n            (country_means['libdem_2020s'] >= 0.5)\n        ) | (\n            (country_means['libdem_1990s'] >= 0.5) & \n            (country_means['libdem_2020s'] >= 0.5)\n        )\n    ]['country'].tolist()\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Expanded democratizer list: {len(democratizers)} countries\")\n    return democratizers\n\n\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Comprehensive Dual Stratification Dataset Creation\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Step 1: Load V-Dem data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 1: Loading V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_df = load_vdem_data()\n    \n    if vdem_df is None:\n        logger.error(\"Could not load V-Dem data. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Step 2: Expand democratizer list\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 2: Expanding democratizer list...\")\n    democratizers = expand_democratizer_list(vdem_df)\n    \n    if not democratizers:\n        logger.error(\"No democratizers found. Exiting.\")\n        return\n    \n    # Limit to top 40 for manageability\n    if len(democratizers) > 40:\n        democratizers = democratizers[:40]\n        logger.info(f\"Limited to 40 countries\")\n    \n    # Step 3: Filter V-Dem to democratizers and 1990-2024\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 3: Filtering V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_filtered = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) &\n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 2024)) &\n        (vdem_df['estimate'] == 'best')\n    ].copy()\n    \n    # Select and rename columns\n    vdem_subset = vdem_filtered[['country', 'year', 'libdem_vdem', 'egaldem_vdem']].copy()\n    vdem_subset = vdem_subset.rename(columns={\n        'libdem_vdem': 'v2x_libdem',\n        'egaldem_vdem': 'v2pepwrsoc'\n    })\n    \n    logger.info(f\"V-Dem subset: {len(vdem_subset)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 4: Download Gini from World Bank\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 4: Downloading Gini coefficient from World Bank...\")\n    gini_df = download_world_bank_indicator('SI.POV.GINI')\n    \n    if gini_df is not None:\n        # Filter to democratizers\n        gini_filtered = gini_df[gini_df['country'].isin(democratizers)].copy()\n        gini_filtered = gini_filtered.rename(columns={'value': 'gini'})\n        logger.info(f\"Gini data: {len(gini_filtered)} records\")\n    else:\n        gini_filtered = None\n    \n    # Step 5: Download education spending from World Bank\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 5: Downloading education spending from World Bank...\")\n    edu_spend_df = download_world_bank_indicator('SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS')\n    \n    if edu_spend_df is not None:\n        # Filter to democratizers\n        edu_spend_filtered = edu_spend_df[edu_spend_df['country'].isin(democratizers)].copy()\n        edu_spend_filtered = edu_spend_filtered.rename(columns={'value': 'education_spending_gdp'})\n        logger.info(f\"Education spending data: {len(edu_spend_filtered)} records\")\n    else:\n        edu_spend_filtered = None\n    \n    # Step 6: Load education enrollment data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 6: Loading education enrollment data...\")\n    \n    # Try to load from OWID\n    edu_enroll_path = OWID_TABLES_DIR / \"full_garden_education_2023-07-17_education_lee_lee_education_lee_lee.json\"\n    \n    if edu_enroll_path.exists():\n        with open(edu_enroll_path, 'r') as f:\n            edu_enroll_data = json.load(f)\n        \n        edu_enroll_df = pd.DataFrame(edu_enroll_data)\n        logger.info(f\"Loaded education enrollment: {len(edu_enroll_df)} rows\")\n        \n        # Filter to democratizers\n        edu_enroll_filtered = edu_enroll_df[\n            (edu_enroll_df['country'].isin(democratizers)) &\n            (edu_enroll_df['year'].between(1990, 2024))\n        ].copy()\n        \n        # Find tertiary enrollment column\n        enroll_cols = [col for col in edu_enroll_filtered.columns if 'tertiary' in col.lower()]\n        if enroll_cols:\n            enroll_col = enroll_cols[0]\n            logger.info(f\"Found tertiary enrollment column: {enroll_col}\")\n            edu_enroll_filtered = edu_enroll_filtered.rename(columns={enroll_col: 'tertiary_enrollment'})\n            edu_enroll_subset = edu_enroll_filtered[['country', 'year', 'tertiary_enrollment']].copy()\n        else:\n            edu_enroll_subset = None\n    else:\n        edu_enroll_subset = None\n    \n    # Step 7: Merge all datasets\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 7: Merging datasets...\")\n    \n    # Start with V-Dem as master\n    master_df = vdem_subset.copy()\n    \n    # Merge Gini\n    if gini_filtered is not None:\n        master_df = master_df.merge(\n            gini_filtered[['country', 'year', 'gini']],\n            on=['country', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"After merging Gini: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Merge education spending\n    if edu_spend_filtered is not None:\n        master_df = master_df.merge(\n            edu_spend_filtered[['country', 'year', 'education_spending_gdp']],\n            on=['country', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"After merging education spending: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Merge education enrollment\n    if edu_enroll_subset is not None:\n        master_df = master_df.merge(\n            edu_enroll_subset,\n            on=['country', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"After merging tertiary enrollment: {len(master_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Step 8: Create education inequality proxy\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 8: Creating education inequality proxy...\")\n    \n    if 'tertiary_enrollment' in master_df.columns:\n        # Standardize (higher enrollment = less inequality)\n        valid_mask = master_df['tertiary_enrollment'].notna()\n        if valid_mask.sum() > 10:  # Need at least 10 valid values\n            master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z'] = np.nan\n            master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment_z'] = (\n                master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'] -\n                master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'].mean()\n            ) / master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'].std()\n            \n            # Create index (higher = more inequality)\n            master_df['edu_ineq_index'] = -master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z']\n            logger.info(\"Education inequality index created\")\n    \n    # Step 9: Add metadata\n    master_df['post_1990_democratizer'] = master_df['country'].isin(democratizers)\n    \n    # Step 10: Save output\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 9: Saving output files...\")\n    \n    # Convert to records\n    records = master_df.to_dict('records')\n    \n    # Save full dataset\n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(records)} records to data_out.json\")\n    \n    # Save mini version (3 records)\n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_mini.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records[:3] if len(records) >= 3 else records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(\"Saved data_out_mini.json\")\n    \n    # Save preview version\n    preview_records = []\n    for r in records[:3]:\n        preview_r = {k: (str(v)[:50] if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in r.items()}\n        preview_records.append(preview_r)\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_preview.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(preview_records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(\"Saved data_out_preview.json\")\n    \n    # Step 11: Generate data quality report\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 10: Generating data quality report...\")\n    \n    report = {\n        'total_rows': len(master_df),\n        'total_countries': master_df['country'].nunique(),\n        'year_range': (int(master_df['year'].min()), int(master_df['year'].max())),\n        'columns': list(master_df.columns),\n        'missing_data': {\n            col: {\n                'count': int(master_df[col].isna().sum()),\n                'percent': float(master_df[col].isna().mean() * 100)\n            }\n            for col in master_df.columns\n            if master_df[col].isna().any()\n        }\n    }\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_quality_report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    # Print summary\n    logger.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(\"Dataset creation complete!\")\n    logger.info(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Rows: {report['total_rows']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Countries: {report['total_countries']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Years: {report['year_range'][0]}-{report['year_range'][1]}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Columns: {len(report['columns'])}\")\n    \n    if report['missing_data']:\n        logger.info(\"\\nMissing data summary:\")\n        for col, info in report['missing_data'].items():\n            logger.info(f\"  {col}: {info['count']} ({info['percent']:.1f}%)\")\n    \n    # Print sample of countries\n    logger.info(f\"\\nSample countries: {list(master_df['country'].unique())[:10]}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n", "kind": "FileEditorObservation"}

  "file_text": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create FINAL dual stratification dataset with proper democratizer list.\n\nThis is the final version that:\n1. Uses correct post-1990 democratizer countries only\n2. Has all required variables (v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc, gini, education variables)\n3. Attempts to minimize missing data\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Setup logging\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format='%(asctime)s|%(levelname)-6s|%(message)s',\n    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nOWID_TABLES_DIR = Path(\"/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/.claude/skills/aii-owid-datasets/temp/tables\")\n\n\ndef identify_true_democratizers(vdem_df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Identify TRUE post-1990 democratizers.\n    \n    These are countries that were NOT democratic in 1990 but became democratic by 1995.\n    Excludes established democracies like Australia, Canada, etc.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter to best estimates\n    vdem_best = vdem_df[\n        (vdem_df['estimate'] == 'best') &\n        (vdem_df['year'].between(1990, 1995))\n    ].copy()\n    \n    democratizers = []\n    \n    for country in vdem_best['country'].unique():\n        country_data = vdem_best[vdem_best['country'] == country].sort_values('year')\n        \n        # Get early 1990s values\n        v1990 = country_data[country_data['year'] == 1990]['libdem_vdem'].values\n        v1995 = country_data[country_data['year'] == 1995]['libdem_vdem'].values\n        \n        # Need both data points\n        if len(v1990) == 0 or len(v1995) == 0:\n            continue\n        \n        # True democratizer: was NOT democratic in 1990 (libdem < 0.5)\n        # and became democratic by 1995 (libdem >= 0.5)\n        if v1990[0] < 0.5 and v1995[0] >= 0.5:\n            democratizers.append(country)\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Identified {len(democratizers)} TRUE post-1990 democratizers\")\n    return democratizers\n\n\ndef impute_missing_data(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Attempt to impute missing data using simple methods.\"\"\"\n    df_imputed = df.copy()\n    \n    # For each numeric column, try forward/backward fill by country\n    numeric_cols = ['gini', 'education_spending_gdp', 'tertiary_enrollment']\n    \n    for col in numeric_cols:\n        if col in df_imputed.columns:\n            # Group by country and interpolate\n            df_imputed[col] = df_imputed.groupby('country')[col].apply(\n                lambda x: x.interpolate(method='linear').fillna(method='ffill').fillna(method='bfill')\n            )\n            \n            filled = df_imputed[col].notna().sum() - df[col].notna().sum()\n            if filled > 0:\n                logger.info(f\"Imputed {filled} missing values for {col}\")\n    \n    return df_imputed\n\n\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"FINAL Dual Stratification Dataset Creation\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    \n    # Step 1: Load V-Dem data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 1: Loading V-Dem data...\")\n    vdem_path = OWID_TABLES_DIR / \"full_garden_democracy_2024-03-07_vdem_vdem_multi_with_regions.json\"\n    \n    with open(vdem_path, 'r') as f:\n        vdem_data = json.load(f)\n    \n    vdem_df = pd.DataFrame(vdem_data)\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded V-Dem: {len(vdem_df)} rows\")\n    \n    # Extract Gini, education data\n            gini_cols = ['country', 'year', 'gini', 'education_spending_gdp', 'tertiary_enrollment']\n            existing_subset = existing_df[gini_cols].copy()\n            \n            # Step 5: Create final merged dataset\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 5: Creating final merged dataset...\")\n    \n    # Start with V-Dem\n    master_df = vdem_subset.copy()\n    \n    # Merge Gini and education data\n    if gini_df is not None:\n        master_df = master_df.merge(\n            gini_df,\n            on=['country', 'year'],\n            how='left'\n        )\n    \n    # Step 6: Impute missing data\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 6: Imputing missing data...\")\n    master_df = impute_missing_data(master_df)\n    \n    # Step 7: Recreate education inequality index\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 7: Creating education inequality proxy...\")\n    \n    if 'tertiary_enrollment' in master_df.columns:\n        valid_mask = master_df['tertiary_enrollment'].notna()\n        if valid_mask.sum() > 10:\n            master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z'] = np.nan\n            master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment_z'] = (\n                master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'] -\n                master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'].mean()\n            ) / master_df.loc[valid_mask, 'tertiary_enrollment'].std()\n            \n            master_df['edu_ineq_index'] = -master_df['tertiary_enrollment_z']\n            logger.info(\"Education inequality index created\")\n    \n    # Step 8: Add metadata\n    master_df['post_1990_democratizer'] = True  # All countries in this dataset are democratizers\n    \n    # Step 9: Final data quality check\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 8: Final data quality check...\")\n    \n    n_rows = len(master_df)\n    n_countries = master_df['country'].nunique()\n    n_missing = {col: master_df[col].isna().sum() for col in master_df.columns if master_df[col].isna().any()}\n    \n    logger.info(f\"Final dataset: {n_rows} rows, {n_countries} countries\")\n    logger.info(\"Missing data:\")\n    for col, n in n_missing.items():\n        logger.info(f\"  {col}: {n} ({n/n_rows*100:.1f}%)\")\n    \n    # Step 10: Save output files\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 9: Saving output files...\")\n    \n    # Convert to records\n    records = master_df.to_dict('records')\n    \n    # Save full dataset\n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(records)} records to data_out.json\")\n    \n    # Save complete cases version (no missing data)\n    complete_df = master_df.dropna()\n    complete_records = complete_df.to_dict('records')\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_complete.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(complete_records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(f\"Saved {len(complete_records)} complete cases to data_out_complete.json\")\n    \n    # Save preview version\n    preview_records = []\n    for r in records[:3]:\n        preview_r = {k: (str(v)[:50] if isinstance(v, str) else v) for k, v in r.items()}\n        preview_records.append(preview_r)\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'data_out_preview.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(preview_records, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    logger.info(\"Saved data_out_preview.json\")\n    \n    # Step 11: Generate final report\n    logger.info(\"\\nStep 10: Generating final report...\")\n    \n    report = {\n        'total_rows': n_rows,\n        'total_countries': n_countries,\n        'year_range': (int(master_df['year'].min()), int(master_df['year'].max())),\n        'countries': list(master_df['country'].unique()),\n        'columns': list(master_df.columns),\n        'missing_data': {\n            col: {\n                'count': int(n),\n                'percent': float(n / n_rows * 100)\n            }\n            for col, n in n_missing.items()\n        },\n        'complete_cases': len(complete_records)\n    }\n    \n    with open(OUTPUT_DIR / 'dataset_report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, default=str)\n    \n    # Print summary\n    logger.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(\"FINAL Dataset Creation Complete!\")\n    logger.info(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Rows: {report['total_rows']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Countries: {report['total_countries']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Years: {report['year_range'][0]}-{report['year_range'][1]}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Complete cases: {report['complete_cases']}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Columns: {len(report['columns'])}\")\n    logger.info(f\"\\nCountries: {report['countries']}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",

<artifact_plan>
id: gen_plan_dataset_1_idx1
type: dataset
title: 'Dual Stratification Dataset: Post-1990 Democratizers Panel (1990-2024)'
summary: >-
  Comprehensive merged panel dataset combining V-Dem political institutions, World Bank PIP income inequality, UNESCO education
  indicators, and education spending data for testing the dual stratification hypothesis.
runpod_compute_profile: cpu_light
ideal_dataset_criteria: >-
  Balanced panel dataset covering 35-40 post-1990 democratizer countries from 1990-2024. Each country-year observation must
  contain: (1) V-Dem liberal democracy index (v2x_libdem, 0-1) and political equality index (v2pepwrsoc, 0-4); (2) Income
  inequality via Gini coefficient (World Bank PIP); (3) Education inequality proxied by tertiary enrollment rates (%) and
  mean years of schooling (years); (4) Public education spending as % of GDP. Dataset should have <30% missing data overall,
  with key variables having <20% missing. Data must be mergeable on country-year keys. Output as JSON in data_out.json with
  schema containing country, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc, gini, tertiary_enrollment, mean_years_schooling, education_spending_gdp
  fields. Include full/mini/preview versions. File size <300MB.
dataset_search_plan: |-
  PHASE 1: DOWNLOAD RAW DATASETS (Parallel Execution)

  1.1 V-Dem v.14 Country-Year Dataset
  PRIMARY SOURCE: https://v-dem.net/static/website/data/archive/v14/V-Dem-CY+Other-CS-Coding_14.csv
  Size: ~200MB, contains 500+ variables for 200+ countries (1789-2024)
  Extract columns: country_name, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc
  V-Dem Codebook v.14 defines v2pepwrsoc as Political equality index (0=elite monopoly, 4=equal power)
  FALLBACK: If CSV download fails, use aii-hf-datasets to search 'v-dem democracy' OR download Quality of Government (QoG) Standard Dataset from http://www.qog.pol.gu.se/data/qog_basic

  1.2 Identify Post-1990 Democratizers
  Method: Filter V-Dem data to countries where v2x_libdem transitions from <0.5 to >=0.5 during 1990-1995
  Expected countries (35-45): Post-communist Europe (Poland, Hungary, Czech Republic, Slovakia, Estonia, Latvia, Lithuania, Slovenia, Croatia, Romania, Bulgaria, Albania, North Macedonia, Serbia, Montenegro, Bosnia-Herzegovina, Kosovo, Ukraine, Georgia, Armenia, Moldova, Kyrgyzstan, Mongolia), Latin America (Brazil, Argentina, Peru, Ecuador, Bolivia, Paraguay, El Salvador, Guatemala, Honduras, Nicaragua), Sub-Saharan Africa (South Africa, Ghana, Benin, Mali, Malawi, Zambia, Tanzania, Senegal, Kenya, Nigeria, Uganda, Lesotho, Madagascar), Asia (Indonesia, Philippines, Thailand, South Korea, Taiwan)
  CODE: pandas read_csv with low_memory=False, filter to 1990-2024, identify democratizers

  1.3 World Bank PIP Gini Coefficients
  PRIMARY: World Bank Open Data API for indicator SI.POV.GINI
  URL: https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/SI.POV.GINI?format=csv&per_page=10000&date=1990:2024
  NOTE: Skip first row (metadata) when reading CSV
  FALLBACK 1: SWIID (Standardized World Income Inequality Database) - https://fsolt.org/swiid/ - download CSV from website
  FALLBACK 2: UNU-WIDER WIID database - https://www.wider.unu.edu/database/wiid
  Expected: Gini coefficients (0-100 scale) for 35-40 countries, 1990-2024

  1.4 UNESCO Education Data (Two Proxies)
  Proxy A: Tertiary Enrollment Rates
  SOURCE: UNESCO UIS - Indicator SE.TER.ENRR (Gross enrollment ratio, tertiary, both sexes %)
  URL: http://data.uis.unesco.org/ (use query tool, export CSV)
  FALLBACK: Our World in Data - use aii-owid-datasets search 'tertiary education enrollment'

  Proxy B: Mean Years of Schooling
  SOURCE 1: Barro-Lee Dataset v3.0 - http://www.barrolee.com/data/bl_v3.zip
  File: bl_v3.dta (Stata) - use pandas.read_stata() to convert
  Variable: total_edYrs (mean years, age 15+ or 25+)
  Coverage: 146 countries, 1950-2015 (supplement with newer UNESCO data to 2024)
  SOURCE 2: UNDP HDR API - https://hdr.undp.org/data-center/statistical-data-api
  FALLBACK: OWID 'mean years of schooling' - search via aii-owid-datasets

  1.5 Education Spending (% of GDP)
  PRIMARY: World Bank WDI indicator SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS (Government expenditure on education, total % of GDP)
  URL: https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/SE.XPD.TOTL.GD.ZS?format=csv&per_page=10000&date=1990:2024
  SECONDARY: UNESCO UIS indicator X_DC_PPP (public expenditure on education as % of GDP)
  TERTIARY: OECD SOCX - https://www.oecd.org/social/expenditure.htm (SOCX_2023_CSV.zip)

  PHASE 2: DATA CLEANING AND MERGING

  2.1 Country Name Standardization
  Problem: V-Dem, World Bank, UNESCO use different country names
  Solution: Use V-Dem country_name as master, create crosswalk dictionary for common variants
  Use ISO3 codes as merge key (more stable than names) - add using pycountry library
  CODE: Create dictionary mapping variants, e.g., {'United States': 'United States of America', 'Russia': 'Russian Federation'}

  2.2 Merge Strategy
  Start with V-Dem data filtered to post-1990 democratizers (master)
  Left join Gini by (country_name, year)
  Left join tertiary enrollment by (country_name, year)
  Left join mean years schooling by (country_name, year)
  Left join education spending by (country_name, year)
  CODE: pandas merge with how='left', validate='m:1'

  2.3 Create Education Inequality Index (Proxy)
  Since direct education inequality measures by SES unavailable, create proxy:
  Standardize tertiary_enrollment and mean_years_schooling (z-scores)
  edu_ineq_index = -tertiary_enrollment_z - mean_years_schooling_z (higher = more inequality)
  CODE: from scipy.stats import zscore; master_df['edu_ineq_index'] = -zscore(tertiary) - zscore(mean_years)

  PHASE 3: HANDLE MISSING DATA

  3.1 Missing Data Analysis
  Generate missing data heatmap (countries x years)
  Calculate % missing by variable and by country
  Identify if missing At Random (MAR) or Not At Random (MNAR)

  3.2 Imputation
  Option A: MICE (Multiple Imputation via Chained Equations) using sklearn IterativeImputer
  Option B: Listwise deletion (complete cases only) for robustness checks
  Recommendation: Create TWO versions - imputed and complete cases
  CODE: from sklearn.experimental import enable_iterative_imputer; IterativeImputer(max_iter=10, random_state=42)

  PHASE 4: EXPLORATORY DATA ANALYSIS (EDA)

  4.1 Descriptive Statistics
  Summary stats table (N, mean, SD, min, max, median) for all continuous variables
  Save to eda_descriptive_stats.csv

  4.2 Correlation Matrix
  Compute Pearson and Spearman correlations
  Test hypothesis preview: correlation of (Gini x edu_ineq_index) with v2x_libdem
  Save to eda_correlation_matrix.csv

  4.3 Temporal Plots
  Line plot: v2x_libdem trajectories by country (spaghetti plot)
  Line plot: Gini coefficient trends
  Save to eda_figures/ (PNG format)

  4.4 Data Quality Checks
  Validate ranges: v2x_libdem [0-1], v2pepwrsoc [0-4], Gini [20-70]
  Check for duplicates: assert no duplicate (country, year) pairs
  Check N countries: assert >=30
  Save to data_quality_report.txt

  PHASE 5: OUTPUT FILES

  5.1 Final Dataset (JSON)
  Format: JSON Lines (one JSON object per line) for efficient streaming
  Schema: {country, year, v2x_libdem, v2pepwrsoc, gini, tertiary_enrollment, mean_years_schooling, education_spending_gdp, edu_ineq_index, post_1990_democratizer, democratization_year}
  Save as data_out.json (full), data_out_mini.json (3 rows), data_out_preview.json (truncated for logging)

  5.2 Validation
  Use aii-json skill to validate JSON schema
  Use aii-file-size-limit to check file size <300MB

  5.3 Documentation
  Create dataset_documentation.md with: data sources/URLs, variable definitions, country list, missing data summary, EDA references

  FALLBACK PLANS:
  If V-Dem fails: Use QoG dataset or Polity V (https://www.systemicpeace.org/polityproject.html)
  If WB Gini fails: Use SWIID or UNU-WIDER WIID
  If UNESCO fails: Use Barro-Lee or Penn World Table 10.0
  If education spending fails: Use IMF GFS or impute from GDPpc

  TIME BUDGET (6 hours total):
  Download: 1h (parallelize with asyncio)
  Clean/merge: 2h
  Missing data: 1h
  EDA/validation: 1.5h
  Documentation: 0.5h

  Use aii-parallel-computing skill for parallel downloads and imputation.
target_num_datasets: 4
</artifact_plan>
